--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/UserRole.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include <QString>

// Who is using the terminal. Chosen once at start-up.
enum class UserRole
{
    Cashier, // records orders, generates bills, views products
    Manager  // views products only (Products page + scanning a barcode for its description)
};

// Single place that decides what each role may open (SRP).
// Adding a role or changing a permission only touches this file (OCP for the rest of the UI).
// Page numbers are MainWindow::Page values:
// Dashboard 0, RecordOrder 1, Orders 2, Products 3, Scanner 4, Bills 5, Settings 6.
namespace RolePolicy
{
inline bool canOpen(UserRole role, int page)
{
    if (role == UserRole::Cashier)
        return true;
    return page == 3 || page == 4; // Manager: Products, Barcode Scanner
}

// First page shown after signing in.
inline int startPage(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? 3 : 1;
}

inline QString name(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? QStringLiteral("Manager") : QStringLiteral("Cashier");
}
} // namespace RolePolicy

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/legacy-camera/CameraWidget.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgcodecs.hpp>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this)), status(new QLabel("Camera is off", this)),
      toggle(new QPushButton("Start camera", this)), upload(new QPushButton("Upload image", this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    // NEW: buttons + status label under the picture
    auto *buttons = new QHBoxLayout();
    buttons->addWidget(toggle);
    buttons->addWidget(upload);
    Layout->addLayout(buttons);
    Layout->addWidget(status);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
    connect(toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onToggle);
    connect(upload, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onUpload);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
    {
        timer.start(30);
        toggle->setText("Stop camera");
        setStatus("Point a barcode at the camera");
    }
    else
    {
        // NEW: error handling - tell the user, and offer the upload instead
        const QString message = QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage());
        setStatus(message + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
    scanner.closeCamera(); // NEW: really release the camera, otherwise its light stays on
    toggle->setText("Start camera");
    setStatus("Camera is off");
}
// NEW: Start / Stop button
void CameraWidget::onToggle()
{
    if (timer.isActive())
        stop();
    else
        start();
}
// NEW: upload fallback, choose a picture of a barcode
void CameraWidget::onUpload()
{
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(this, "Choose a barcode picture", QString(),
                                                      "Pictures (*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.bmp)");
    if (!path.isEmpty())
        scanImage(path);
}
void CameraWidget::scanImage(const QString &path)
{
    QFile file(path); // read the bytes ourselves, so file names with special letters also work on Windows
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
    {
        setStatus("Cannot open the picture");
        emit scanError("Cannot open the picture");
        return;
    }
    const QByteArray bytes = file.readAll();
    const cv::Mat picture = cv::imdecode(cv::Mat(1, bytes.size(), CV_8UC1, (void *)bytes.constData()), cv::IMREAD_COLOR);
    if (picture.empty())
    {
        setStatus("This file is not a picture");
        emit scanError("This file is not a picture");
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect box;
    if (scanner.frameRead(six, box, picture))
    {
        setStatus("Scanned " + QString::fromStdString(six));
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
    else
    {
        const QString message = scanner.badCheckDigit() ? "Wrong check digit in the picture" : "No barcode found in the picture";
        setStatus(message);
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::setStatus(const QString &text)
{
    status->setText(text);
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        stop();
        setStatus(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()) + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        // NEW: duplicate-scan prevention - the same barcode still in front of the camera is ignored for 2 seconds
        const QString code = QString::fromStdString(six);
        if (code == lastCode && lastClock.isValid() && lastClock.elapsed() < 2000)
            return;
        lastCode = code;
        lastClock.restart();
        setStatus("Scanned " + code);
        emit barcodeScan(code);
    }
    else if (scanner.badCheckDigit())
        setStatus("Wrong check digit - hold the barcode steady");
}

#pragma once
#include <QWidget>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QString>
#include <QImage>
#include <opencv2/core.hpp>
#include "cameraScanner.h"
class QLabel;
class CameraWidget : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit CameraWidget(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void start();
    void stop();
signals:
    void barcodeScan(const QString &six);
    void scanError(const QString &message);
private slots:
    void onTick();

private:
    // OpenCV picture to Qt picture
    static QImage toQImage(constcv::Mat &BGR);
    // backend work of decoding and scanning
    cameraScanner scanner;
    QLabel *view;
    QTimer timer;
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/legacy-camera/main.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/AppState.cpp

#include "AppState.h"
#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"
#include "billing/BillingService.h"
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFile>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QtMath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>

#ifndef BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR
#define BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR ""
#endif

ProductInfo ProductInfo::from(const Product& p, const QString& rootDir)
{
    ProductInfo i;
    i.id = p.getProductID();
    i.sku = QString::fromStdString(p.getAcronym());
    i.ean = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcode());
    i.name = QString::fromStdString(p.getProductName());
    i.unit = QString::fromStdString(p.getMeasurementUnitName());
    i.price = p.getUnitPrice();
    i.tax = p.getTax();
    i.discount = p.getDiscount();
    i.stock = p.getAvailableQuantity();
    const QString path = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcodeFilePath());
    i.imagePath = QFileInfo(path).isAbsolute() ? path : QDir(rootDir).filePath(path);
    return i;
}

int OrderRecord::itemCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        n += l.qty;
    return n;
}

void OrderRecord::recompute()
{
    subtotal = discount = tax = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
    {
        subtotal += l.price * l.qty;
        discount += l.discount * l.qty;
        tax += l.tax * l.qty;
    }
    total = subtotal - discount + tax;
}

QString OrderRecord::summary() const
{
    if (lines.isEmpty())
        return "-";
    QStringList names;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        names << l.name;
    QString s = QString("%1 item%2  ").arg(itemCount()).arg(itemCount() == 1 ? "" : "s");
    s += names.mid(0, 2).join(", ");
    if (names.size() > 2)
        s += QString(", +%1").arg(names.size() - 2);
    return s;
}

AppState& AppState::instance()
{
    static AppState s;
    return s;
}

QString AppState::locateDataDir()
{
    QStringList candidates;
    const QByteArray env = qgetenv("BARCODEHUB_DATA");
    if (!env.isEmpty())
        candidates << QString::fromLocal8Bit(env);
    const QString app = QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath();
    candidates << app + "/data" << app + "/../data" << app + "/../../data" << app + "/../../../data"
        << QDir::currentPath() + "/data" << QString(BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR) + "/data";
    for (const auto& c : candidates)
        if (QFileInfo::exists(c + "/Product.txt"))
            return QDir::cleanPath(c);
    return QString();
}

AppState::AppState()
{
    const QString dir = locateDataDir();
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
        loadProducts(dir + "/Product.txt");
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
    {
        const std::string root = dir.toStdString();
        m_orderManager = std::make_unique<barcodehub::order::OrderManager>(root + "/Order.txt", root + "/OrderDetail.txt");
        m_billRepository = std::make_unique<barcodehub::billing::BillRepository>(root + "/Bill.txt");
        refreshOrders();
    }
}

bool AppState::loadProducts(const QString& path)
{
    m_root = QDir::cleanPath(QFileInfo(path).absolutePath() + "/..");
    m_productPath = path;
    const bool ok = m_manager.loadProducts(path.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    emit changed();
    return ok;
}

void AppState::refreshCache()
{
    m_products.clear();
    for (const auto& p : m_manager.getProducts())
        m_products.push_back(ProductInfo::from(p, m_root));
}

QStringList AppState::productIssues() const
{
    QStringList out;
    const auto& r = m_manager.getLastLoad();
    if (!r.fileOpened)
        out << QString::fromStdString(r.summary());
    for (const auto& i : r.issues)
        if (i.line > 0)
            out << QString("%1 line %2: %3")
            .arg(i.severity == IssueSeverity::Error ? "Error  " : "Warning")
            .arg(i.line)
            .arg(QString::fromStdString(i.message));
    return out;
}

std::optional<ProductInfo> AppState::findByCode(const QString& code) const
{
    if (const Product* p = m_manager.searchByCode(code.trimmed().toStdString()))
        return ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root);
    return std::nullopt;
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::searchText(const QString& query) const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> out;
    for (const Product* p : m_manager.searchByText(query.trimmed().toStdString()))
        out.push_back(ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root));
    return out;
}

ProductDescriptionView AppState::describe(int productId) const
{
    ProductDescriptionView v;
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return v;
    const ProductDescription d = ProductDescriber(m_settings.lowStockThreshold).describe(*p);
    v.title = QString::fromStdString(d.title);
    v.level = QString::fromStdString(d.stockLevel);
    for (const auto& f : d.fields)
        v.fields.push_back({ QString::fromStdString(f.first), QString::fromStdString(f.second) });
    v.imagePath = ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root).imagePath;
    return v;
}

QString AppState::stockProblem(int productId, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const
{
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return QString(); // custom lines are not stock-controlled
    const StockCheck c = m_stock.checkAdd(*p, alreadyInOrder, toAdd);
    return c.ok() ? QString() : QString::fromStdString(c.message);
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::lowStock() const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> v;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        if (p.stock <= m_settings.lowStockThreshold)
            v << p;
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end(), [](const ProductInfo& a, const ProductInfo& b) { return a.stock < b.stock; });
    return v;
}

void AppState::reorder(int id)
{
    m_reordered.insert(id);
    emit changed();
}

void AppState::reorderAllLow()
{
    for (const auto& p : lowStock())
        m_reordered.insert(p.id);
    emit changed();
}

QString AppState::nextOrderId()
{
    if (!m_orderManager)
        return "--------";
    std::string error;
    const std::string id = m_orderManager->nextOrderId(&error);
    return id.empty() ? QString("--------") : QString::fromStdString(id);
}

bool AppState::addOrder(OrderRecord& o, QString* error)
{
    auto fail = [&](const QString& why) {
        if (error) *error = why;
        return false;
        };
    if (error) error->clear();
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository)
        return fail("Data directory is unavailable. Check BARCODEHUB_DATA and Product.txt.");
    if (o.lines.isEmpty())
        return fail("The order is empty.");

    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    std::string why;
    bh::order::PaymentMode mode = bh::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    if (!bh::order::paymentModeFromString(o.payment.toLower().toStdString(), mode))
        return fail("Unsupported payment method.");
    bh::billing::PaymentRequest payment;
    payment.mode = mode;
    payment.merchant = o.merchant.toStdString();
    payment.amountTendered = o.tendered;
    const auto methods = bh::billing::PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults();
    const auto* method = methods.find(mode);
    if (!method)
        return fail("Payment method unavailable.");

    m_orderManager->clearCart();
    std::map<std::string, bh::order::ProductSnapshot> manualProducts;
    std::map<int, int> stockToDeduct;
    for (const auto& line : o.lines)
    {
        bh::order::ProductSnapshot snapshot;
        if (line.productId > 0)
        {
            const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(line.productId);
            if (!p || QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) != line.acronym)
                return fail("A product in the order no longer exists. Refresh and retry.");
            snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p);
            stockToDeduct[line.productId] += line.qty;
        }
        else
        {
            // Manual items have no inventory entry. Their snapshot remains valid for this
            // checkout and their price/quantity is saved to Bill.txt for future receipts.
            snapshot.productId = "CUSTOM-" + line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.barcode = snapshot.productId;
            snapshot.acronym = line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.name = line.name.toStdString();
            snapshot.measurementUnit = "count";
            snapshot.unitPrice = line.price;
            snapshot.taxPerUnit = line.tax;
            snapshot.discountPerUnit = line.discount;
            snapshot.availableQuantity = 1000000;
            manualProducts[snapshot.productId] = snapshot;
        }
        if (!m_orderManager->addToCart(snapshot, line.qty, &why))
            return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));
    }
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        if (const auto* p = m_manager.searchByID(id); !p || qty > p->getAvailableQuantity())
            return fail("Stock changed while preparing checkout. Refresh and retry.");

    // Validate the same arithmetic/payment rules used by Billing before touching disk.
    o.recompute();
    const double payable = m_orderManager->totalPayable();
    if (std::fabs(o.total - payable) > 0.011)
        return fail("Cart totals differ from backend totals. Please refresh the order.");
    const auto accepted = method->process(payment, payable);
    if (!accepted.ok)
        return fail(QString::fromStdString(accepted.error));

    bh::order::Order created;
    const std::string name = o.customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : o.customer.toStdString();
    const std::string contact = o.contact == "-" ? "" : o.contact.toStdString();
    if (!m_orderManager->createOrder(name, contact, created, &why))
        return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));

    // Capture the original inventory before changing stock, so a failed checkout can
    // restore Product.txt instead of leaving stock deducted for an unpaid order.
    QFile stockFile(m_productPath);
    if (!stockFile.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
    {
        std::string ignored;
        m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        return fail("Cannot back up Product.txt before checkout.");
    }
    const QByteArray originalStock = stockFile.readAll();
    stockFile.close();
    auto restoreStock = [&]() -> bool {
        QSaveFile output(m_productPath);
        if (!output.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) return false;
        if (output.write(originalStock) != originalStock.size()) return false;
        if (!output.commit()) return false;
        m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
        refreshCache();
        return true;
        };
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        m_manager.deductStock(id, qty);
    if (!m_manager.saveProducts(&why))
    {
        const bool restored = restoreStock();
        std::string ignored;
        const bool discarded = m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        return fail("Could not save stock: " + QString::fromStdString(why) +
            (!restored || !discarded ? " (rollback needs manual attention)" : ""));
    }

    bh::billing::BillingService service(*m_orderManager, *m_billRepository,
        [this, &manualProducts](const std::string& id, bh::order::ProductSnapshot& snapshot) {
            const auto custom = manualProducts.find(id);
            if (custom != manualProducts.end()) { snapshot = custom->second; return true; }
            try {
                const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(id));
                if (p) { snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p); return true; }
            }
            catch (...) {}
            return false;
        });
    bh::billing::Bill bill;
    double change = 0;
    if (!service.checkout(created.id, payment, bill, &change, &why))
    {
        const bool restored = restoreStock();
        std::string ignored;
        const bool discarded = m_orderManager->discardOrder(created.id, &ignored);
        refreshCache();
        return fail("Checkout failed: " + QString::fromStdString(why) +
            (!restored || !discarded ? " (rollback needs manual attention)" : ""));
    }

    o.id = QString::fromStdString(created.id);
    o.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill.id);
    o.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(created.date + " " + created.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.total = bill.totals.payable;
    o.subtotal = bill.totals.withoutTax;
    o.discount = bill.totals.discount;
    o.tax = bill.totals.tax;
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    // The current session knows the manual item descriptions even if the catalogue doesn't.
    for (auto& history : m_orders)
        if (history.id == o.id) { history = o; break; }
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

void AppState::refreshOrders()
{
    m_orders.clear();
    m_baseSales = 0;
    m_baseOrders = 0;
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository) return;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::Order> orders;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::OrderDetail> details;
    std::vector<barcodehub::billing::Bill> bills;
    std::string error;
    if (!m_orderManager->loadOrders(orders, &error) ||
        !m_orderManager->loadOrderDetails(details, &error) ||
        !m_billRepository->loadAll(bills, &error))
    {
        qWarning("Could not load persisted orders/bills: %s", error.c_str());
        return;
    }
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    for (const auto& entry : orders)
    {
        OrderRecord rec;
        rec.id = QString::fromStdString(entry.id);
        rec.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(entry.date + " " + entry.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
        rec.customer = QString::fromStdString(entry.customerName);
        rec.contact = entry.customerContact.empty() ? "-" : QString::fromStdString(entry.customerContact);
        rec.tier = "Guest";
        rec.shift = "POS #01";
        rec.status = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.status));
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::InProgress) rec.status = "In Progress";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::OnHold) rec.status = "On Hold";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Paid) rec.status = "Paid";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Archived) rec.status = "Archived";
        rec.payment = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.paymentMode));
        rec.merchant = QString::fromStdString(entry.paymentMerchant);
        const auto bill = std::find_if(bills.begin(), bills.end(), [&](const auto& b) { return b.orderId == entry.id; });
        if (bill != bills.end()) rec.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill->id);
        int detailIndex = 0;
        for (const auto& detail : details)
        {
            if (detail.orderId != entry.id) continue;
            const Product* p = nullptr;
            try { p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(detail.productId)); }
            catch (...) {}
            ReceiptLine line;
            line.name = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getProductName()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.acronym = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.productId = p ? p->getProductID() : 0;
            line.qty = static_cast<int>(detail.quantity);
            line.price = p ? p->getUnitPrice() : 0;
            line.tax = p ? p->getTax() : 0;
            line.discount = p ? p->getDiscount() : 0;
            if (bill != bills.end() && detailIndex < static_cast<int>(bill->lines.size()))
            {
                const auto& saved = bill->lines[detailIndex];
                line.acronym = QString::fromStdString(saved.productAcronym);
                if (!p) line.name = line.acronym;
                line.price = saved.unitPrice;
                line.discount = saved.discountPerUnit;
            }
            rec.lines.push_back(line);
            ++detailIndex;
        }
        rec.recompute();
        if (bill != bills.end())
        {
            rec.subtotal = bill->totals.withoutTax;
            rec.discount = bill->totals.discount;
            rec.tax = bill->totals.tax;
            rec.total = bill->totals.payable;
        }
        if (rec.time.date() == today)
        {
            ++m_baseOrders;
            if (rec.status == "Paid") m_baseSales += rec.total;
        }
        m_orders.push_back(std::move(rec));
    }
    std::sort(m_orders.begin(), m_orders.end(), [](const OrderRecord& a, const OrderRecord& b) { return a.time > b.time; });
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/CameraWidget.cpp

#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
        timer.start(30);
    else
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        timer.stop();
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
}

#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "Theme.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QShortcut>
#include <QWidget>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::ui {

DarkModeManager::DarkModeManager(QObject* parent)
    : QObject(parent), m_isDark(Theme::isDark())
{
}

DarkModeManager& DarkModeManager::instance()
{
    static DarkModeManager s_instance;
    return s_instance;
}

bool DarkModeManager::isDarkMode() const
{
    return m_isDark;
}

void DarkModeManager::setDarkMode(bool enable)
{
    if (m_isDark == enable && qApp->styleSheet() == getActiveStyleSheet())
        return;

    m_isDark = enable;
    Theme::setDark(enable);
    applyTheme();
    emit themeChanged(m_isDark);
}

void DarkModeManager::toggle()
{
    setDarkMode(!m_isDark);
}

void DarkModeManager::applyTheme()
{
    if (!qApp)
        return;

    qApp->setStyleSheet(getActiveStyleSheet());

    // Repolish all active top-level windows
    for (QWidget* w : QApplication::topLevelWidgets()) {
        Theme::repolish(w);
        for (QWidget* child : w->findChildren<QWidget*>()) {
            Theme::repolish(child);
        }
    }
}

QString DarkModeManager::buildDarkPaletteStyleSheet() const
{
    return Theme::styleSheet();
}

QString DarkModeManager::getActiveStyleSheet() const
{
    QString base = Theme::styleSheet();

    // Append dedicated styles for the Dark Mode top-bar toggle button
    QString toggleStyle;
    if (m_isDark) {
        toggleStyle = QStringLiteral(R"(
            #darkModeToggle {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #233150, stop:1 #172138);
                border: 1px solid #33446c;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #0f1627;
                border-radius: 12px;
                padding: 5px 12px 5px 10px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12.5px;
                color: #e0e9ff;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:hover {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #2d3e64, stop:1 #1e2a47);
                border-color: #4b629b;
                color: #ffffff;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:pressed {
                border-bottom: 1px solid #0f1627;
                padding-top: 6px;
                padding-bottom: 4px;
                background: #19233a;
            }
        )");
    } else {
        toggleStyle = QStringLiteral(R"(
            #darkModeToggle {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #ffffff, stop:1 #eef3ff);
                border: 1px solid #d9e3f8;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #cbd8f2;
                border-radius: 12px;
                padding: 5px 12px 5px 10px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12.5px;
                color: #1b2a4d;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:hover {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #ffffff, stop:1 #e2ecfd);
                border-color: #b9ccf2;
                color: #0f1b33;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:pressed {
                border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd8f2;
                padding-top: 6px;
                padding-bottom: 4px;
                background: #dce7fb;
            }
        )");
    }

    return base + "\n" + toggleStyle;
}

QIcon DarkModeManager::createSunIcon(const QColor& color, int size)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(size * dpr), int(size * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);

    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(size / 24.0, size / 24.0);

    // Center circular sun disk
    p.setPen(QPen(color, 1.8, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap));
    p.setBrush(QBrush(color));
    p.drawEllipse(QPointF(12.0, 12.0), 4.2, 4.2);

    // 8 radial sun rays
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    const qreal r1 = 6.8;
    const qreal r2 = 9.8;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        const qreal angle = i * (3.14159265358979323846 / 4.0);
        const qreal cosA = std::cos(angle);
        const qreal sinA = std::sin(angle);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(12.0 + r1 * cosA, 12.0 + r1 * sinA),
                   QPointF(12.0 + r2 * cosA, 12.0 + r2 * sinA));
    }

    return QIcon(pm);
}

QIcon DarkModeManager::createMoonIcon(const QColor& color, int size)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(size * dpr), int(size * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);

    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(size / 24.0, size / 24.0);

    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(QBrush(color));

    // Smooth crescent moon geometry
    QPainterPath path;
    path.moveTo(17.5, 4.5);
    path.cubicTo(11.0, 4.5, 5.8, 9.7, 5.8, 16.2);
    path.cubicTo(5.8, 20.0, 7.6, 23.3, 10.4, 25.3);
    path.cubicTo(9.0, 23.2, 8.2, 20.6, 8.2, 17.8);
    path.cubicTo(8.2, 11.5, 13.3, 6.4, 19.6, 6.4);
    path.cubicTo(20.3, 6.4, 21.0, 6.5, 21.6, 6.7);
    path.cubicTo(20.4, 5.3, 19.0, 4.5, 17.5, 4.5);
    path.closeSubpath();

    // Adjust scale/offset to fit 24x24 box
    QTransform tr;
    tr.translate(1.0, -1.5);
    tr.scale(0.9, 0.9);
    p.setTransform(tr);

    p.drawPath(path);

    return QIcon(pm);
}

QPushButton* DarkModeManager::createTopBarToggle(QWidget* parent)
{
    auto* btn = new QPushButton(parent);
    btn->setObjectName("darkModeToggle");
    btn->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);

    auto updateButtonState = [this, btn]() {
        if (m_isDark) {
            btn->setIcon(createSunIcon(QColor("#facc15"), 18)); // Vibrant warm yellow sun
            btn->setText("  Light Mode");
            btn->setToolTip("Switch to Light Theme (Alt+D)");
        } else {
            btn->setIcon(createMoonIcon(QColor("#4338ca"), 18)); // Indigo crescent moon
            btn->setText("  Dark Mode");
            btn->setToolTip("Switch to Dark Theme (Alt+D)");
        }
    };

    updateButtonState();

    connect(btn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &DarkModeManager::toggle);

    connect(this, &DarkModeManager::themeChanged, btn, [updateButtonState]() {
        updateButtonState();
    });

    // Register Alt+D keyboard shortcut
    auto* shortcut = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Alt+D"), btn);
    shortcut->setContext(Qt::ApplicationShortcut);
    connect(shortcut, &QShortcut::activated, this, &DarkModeManager::toggle);

    return btn;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::ui

#include "DashboardPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QLocale>
#include <QScrollArea>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace
{
    // One KPI tile: icon, caption, big number, one supporting line.
    QFrame *statCard(const QString &caption, const QString &icon, const QString &top, const QString &bottom,
                     QLabel *&value, QLabel *&sub, bool alert = false)
    {
        auto *c = W::card(nullptr, alert ? "cardAlert" : "card");
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        h->setSpacing(14);
        h->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, Qt::white, top, bottom, 46));
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(0);
        v->addWidget(W::label(caption, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        if (alert)
            value->setStyleSheet("color:#c0271d;");
        v->addWidget(value);
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        sub->setStyleSheet("font-size:12px;");
        v->addWidget(sub);
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        return c;
    }

    QTableWidget *table(const QStringList &headers)
    {
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(0, headers.size());
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers);
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
        t->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
        t->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setHighlightSections(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        return t;
    }
}

DashboardPage::DashboardPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    build();
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &DashboardPage::refresh);
    refresh();
}

void DashboardPage::focusLookup()
{
    m_search->setFocus();
    m_search->selectAll();
}

void DashboardPage::build()
{
    setObjectName("page");
    auto *outer = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    outer->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    auto *scroll = new QScrollArea;
    scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    scroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    scroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    auto *inner = new QWidget;
    inner->setMinimumHeight(900);
    scroll->setWidget(inner);
    outer->addWidget(scroll);
    auto *root = W::pageLayout(inner);

    // ---- KPI row
    auto *stats = new QHBoxLayout;
    stats->setSpacing(0);
    stats->addWidget(statCard("SALES TODAY", "cash", "#7fb0ff", "#2b5fe8", m_sales, m_salesSub));
    stats->addWidget(statCard("ORDERS", "orders", "#5be3a0", "#15a362", m_orders, m_ordersSub));
    stats->addWidget(statCard("PENDING", "clock", "#ffd479", "#f09a1a", m_pending, m_pendingSub));
    stats->addWidget(statCard("LOW STOCK", "warn", "#ff9a8f", "#d9392b", m_lowCount, m_lowSub, true));
    root->addLayout(stats);

    // ---- quick actions
    auto *qa = new QHBoxLayout;
    qa->setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 4);
    qa->setSpacing(10);
    // (quick buttons removed: the sidebar already opens these pages)
    qa->addStretch(1);
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Find an order or product…  (Ctrl+K)");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(300);
    m_search->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    qa->addWidget(m_search);
    root->addLayout(qa);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, [this]
            { emit lookup(m_search->text()); m_search->clear(); });

    // ---- chart (full width)
    auto *mid = new QHBoxLayout;
    mid->setSpacing(0);
    auto *cc = W::card(this);
    auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(cc);
    cv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 12);
    cv->setSpacing(6);
    auto *legend = new QLabel("Revenue from paid orders");
    legend->setObjectName("muted");
    W::headerRow(cv, "", "Hourly revenue", legend);
    m_chart = new RevenueChart;
    m_chart->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
    m_chart->setData({}, {}, {}); // populated from real paid orders in refresh()
    cv->addWidget(m_chart, 1);
    mid->addWidget(cc, 1);
    root->addLayout(mid, 5);

    // ---- recent orders
    auto *rc = W::card(this);
    auto *rv = new QVBoxLayout(rc);
    rv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 8);
    rv->setSpacing(6);
    auto *all = W::button("View all", "");
    all->setObjectName("link");
    all->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    connect(all, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            { emit navigate(2); });
    W::headerRow(rv, "orders", "Recent orders", all);
    m_recent = table({"ORDER", "TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS", "TOTAL", "STATUS", ""});
    m_recent->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Stretch);
    m_recent->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    m_recent->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_recent->setColumnWidth(6, 120);
    rv->addWidget(m_recent, 1);
    m_showing = W::label("", "muted");
    m_showing->setStyleSheet("font-size:12px;");
    rv->addWidget(m_showing);
    root->addWidget(rc, 6);
}

void DashboardPage::refresh()
{
    auto &st = AppState::instance();
    const auto &orders = st.orders();
    int pending = 0;
    for (const auto &o : orders)
        if (o.status != "Paid")
            ++pending;
    m_sales->setText(W::money(st.salesToday()));
    m_salesSub->setText("Revenue so far today");
    m_orders->setText(QString::number(st.ordersToday()));
    int paidToday = 0;
    for (const auto &o : orders)
        if (o.status == "Paid" && o.time.date() == QDate::currentDate()) ++paidToday;
    m_ordersSub->setText("Average " + W::money(st.salesToday() / qMax(1, paidToday)) + " per paid order");

    QStringList hours;
    QVector<double> earned(13, 0.0), target(13, 0.0);
    for (int hour = 8; hour <= 20; ++hour) hours << QString("%1").arg(hour, 2, 10, QChar('0'));
    for (const auto &o : orders)
        if (o.status == "Paid" && o.time.date() == QDate::currentDate() &&
            o.time.time().hour() >= 8 && o.time.time().hour() <= 20)
            earned[o.time.time().hour() - 8] += o.total;
    m_chart->setData(hours, earned, target);
    m_pending->setText(QString::number(pending));
    m_pendingSub->setText("On hold or in progress");
    const auto low = st.lowStock();
    m_lowCount->setText(QString::number(low.size()));
    m_lowSub->setText("Items at or below the low-stock limit");

    const int m = qMin(5, orders.size());
    m_recent->setRowCount(m);
    for (int r = 0; r < m; ++r)
    {
        const auto &o = orders[r];
        m_recent->setRowHeight(r, 48);
        auto id = new QTableWidgetItem(o.id);
        id->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor("#2554d8")));
        QFont f = id->font();
        f.setBold(true);
        id->setFont(f);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 0, id);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 1, new QTableWidgetItem(o.time.toString("HH:mm")));
        m_recent->setItem(r, 2, new QTableWidgetItem(o.customer));
        m_recent->setItem(r, 3, new QTableWidgetItem(QString::number(o.itemCount())));
        auto *am = new QTableWidgetItem(W::money(o.total));
        am->setFont(f);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 4, am);
        auto *sw = new QWidget;
        auto *swl = new QHBoxLayout(sw);
        swl->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        swl->addWidget(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status)), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        swl->addStretch();
        m_recent->setCellWidget(r, 5, sw);
        auto *ab = W::button("View", "mini");
        auto *aw = new QWidget;
        auto *al = new QHBoxLayout(aw);
        al->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 4, 0);
        al->addWidget(ab);
        m_recent->setCellWidget(r, 6, aw);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        connect(ab, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                { Dialogs::orderDetails(this, copy); });
    }
    m_showing->setText(QString("Showing the latest %1 of %2 saved orders").arg(m).arg(orders.size()));
}

#include "Dialogs.h"

namespace Dialogs
{
    QString receiptText(const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        const int w = 36;
        auto center = [&](const QString &s)
        { return QString((w - s.size()) / 2, ' ') + s; };
        auto two = [&](const QString &l, const QString &r)
        { return l + QString(qMax(1, w - l.size() - r.size()), ' ') + r; };
        const QString rule(w, '-');
        QString t;
        t += center("SYNTRO-CHECK") + "\n" + center("Retail receipt") + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        t += two("Order", o.id) + "\n";
        if (!o.billId.isEmpty()) t += two("Bill", o.billId) + "\n";
        t += two("Date", o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")) + "\n";
        t += two("Customer", o.customer) + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        for (const auto &l : o.lines)
        {
            const double sub = l.price * l.qty;
            QString name = l.name;
            if (name.size() > w - 12)
                name = name.left(w - 13) + "…";
            t += two(QString("%1 x %2").arg(l.qty).arg(name), W::money(sub)) + "\n";
        }
        t += rule + "\n";
        t += two("Subtotal", W::money(o.subtotal)) + "\n";
        if (o.discount > 0)
            t += two("Discount", W::money(-o.discount)) + "\n";
        t += two("Tax", W::money(o.tax)) + "\n";
        t += two("TOTAL", W::money(o.total)) + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        t += two("Payment", o.payment) + "\n";
        if (o.tendered > 0)
        {
            t += two("Tendered", W::money(o.tendered)) + "\n";
            t += two("Change", W::money(o.tendered - o.total)) + "\n";
        }
        t += "\n" + center("Thank you for shopping!") + "\n";
        return t;
    }

    // Bill as a small HTML page: header, details, item table, totals, payment.
    // The same HTML is shown on screen and saved as the PDF, so both always match.
    QString receiptHtml(const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        auto esc = [](const QString &x) { return x.toHtmlEscaped(); };
        auto row = [&](const QString &l, const QString &r, bool strong = false)
        {
            const QString w = strong ? "font-weight:700;" : "";
            return QString("<tr><td style='padding:2px 0;color:#5b6478;%3'>%1</td>"
                           "<td align='right' style='padding:2px 0;%3'>%2</td></tr>").arg(l, r, w);
        };
        QString h;
        h += "<div style='font-family:Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt;color:#1b2b55;'>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin:0;font-size:18pt;font-weight:800;color:#2554d8;'>Syntro-Check</p>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin:0 0 10px 0;color:#5b6478;'>Sales receipt</p>";

        // order details
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0'>";
        h += row("Bill no.", o.billId.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : esc(o.billId));
        h += row("Order no.", esc(o.id));
        h += row("Date", o.time.toString("dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm AP"));
        h += row("Customer", esc(o.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in") : o.customer));
        if (!o.contact.isEmpty())
            h += row("Contact", esc(o.contact));
        h += "</table><hr style='color:#dce4f5;'/>";

        // items
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='3'>"
             "<tr style='background:#eef3ff;color:#2554d8;font-weight:700;'>"
             "<td>Item</td><td align='center'>Qty</td><td align='right'>Price</td>"
             "<td align='right'>Disc.</td><td align='right'>Amount</td></tr>";
        for (const auto &l : o.lines)
        {
            const double amount = (l.price - l.discount) * l.qty;
            h += QString("<tr><td>%1<br/><span style='color:#8a94ab;font-size:8pt;'>%2</span></td>"
                         "<td align='center'>%3</td><td align='right'>%4</td><td align='right'>%5</td>"
                         "<td align='right'>%6</td></tr>")
                     .arg(esc(l.name), esc(l.acronym)).arg(l.qty)
                     .arg(QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(l.price, 'f', 2),
                          l.discount > 0 ? QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(l.discount * l.qty, 'f', 2) : QString("-"),
                          QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(amount, 'f', 2));
        }
        h += "</table><hr style='color:#dce4f5;'/>";

        // totals
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0'>";
        h += row("Subtotal", W::money(o.subtotal));
        h += row("Discount", o.discount > 0 ? W::money(-o.discount) : W::money(0));
        h += row("Tax", W::money(o.tax));
        h += "</table>";
        h += QString("<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='6' style='margin-top:6px;'>"
                     "<tr style='background:#2554d8;color:white;font-weight:800;font-size:13pt;'>"
                     "<td>TOTAL</td><td align='right'>%1</td></tr></table>").arg(W::money(o.total));

        // payment
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' style='margin-top:8px;'>";
        QString mode = o.payment.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : esc(o.payment);
        if (!o.merchant.isEmpty() && o.merchant != "-")
            mode += " (" + esc(o.merchant) + ")";
        h += row("Payment", mode);
        if (o.tendered > 0)
        {
            h += row("Amount received", W::money(o.tendered));
            h += row("Balance returned", W::money(o.tendered - o.total));
        }
        if (!o.status.isEmpty())
            h += row("Status", esc(o.status));
        h += "</table>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin-top:14px;color:#5b6478;'>Thank you for shopping with us!</p></div>";
        return h;
    }

    // Saves the bill as a PDF (A5 page) using Qt's built-in PDF writer.
    bool saveReceiptPdf(const OrderRecord &o, const QString &path)
    {
        QPdfWriter pdf(path);
        pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(QPageSize::A5));
        pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(12, 12, 12, 12), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
        pdf.setTitle("Bill " + (o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId));
        pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
        QTextDocument doc;
        doc.setHtml(receiptHtml(o));
        doc.print(&pdf);
        return QFileInfo(path).exists();
    }

    void receipt(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Bill - " + (o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId));
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(18, 18, 18, 14);
        v->setSpacing(12);
        auto *view = new QTextBrowser;
        view->setHtml(receiptHtml(o));
        view->setStyleSheet("QTextBrowser{background:white;border:1px solid #dce9ff;border-radius:10px;padding:14px;}");
        view->setMinimumSize(470, 600);
        v->addWidget(view);

        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        auto *pdfBtn = W::button("Save as PDF", "soft", "print");
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(pdfBtn);
        h->addStretch();
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        QObject::connect(pdfBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&d, &o]
        {
            const QString name = QString("Bill_%1.pdf").arg(o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId);
            const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(&d, "Save bill as PDF",
                QStandardPaths::writableLocation(QStandardPaths::DocumentsLocation) + "/" + name, "PDF files (*.pdf)");
            if (path.isEmpty())
                return;
            if (saveReceiptPdf(o, path))
                QMessageBox::information(&d, "Bill saved", "Saved to:\n" + QDir::toNativeSeparators(path));
            else
                QMessageBox::warning(&d, "Bill not saved", "Could not write the PDF file.");
        });
        d.exec();
    }

    void orderDetails(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Order " + o.id);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(20, 18, 20, 16);
        v->setSpacing(10);
        auto *top = new QHBoxLayout;
        top->addWidget(W::label("Order " + o.id, "h1"));
        top->addWidget(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status)));
        top->addStretch();
        v->addLayout(top);
        // date • customer • contact (empty parts are skipped, so no stray dots)
        QStringList meta{o.time.toString("dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm AP"), o.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in") : o.customer};
        if (!o.contact.isEmpty()) meta << o.contact;
        v->addWidget(W::label(meta.join("  •  "), "muted"));
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(o.lines.size(), 5);
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels({"ITEM", "QTY", "PRICE", "DISCOUNT", "AMOUNT"});
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::NoSelection);
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::Stretch);
        for (int i = 0; i < o.lines.size(); ++i)
        {
            const auto &l = o.lines[i];
            auto cell = [&](int col, const QString &text, Qt::Alignment a)
            {
                auto *it = new QTableWidgetItem(text);
                it->setTextAlignment(a | Qt::AlignVCenter);
                t->setItem(i, col, it);
            };
            cell(0, l.name, Qt::AlignLeft);
            cell(1, QString::number(l.qty), Qt::AlignCenter);
            cell(2, W::money(l.price), Qt::AlignRight);
            cell(3, l.discount > 0 ? W::money(-l.discount * l.qty) : QString("-"), Qt::AlignRight);
            cell(4, W::money((l.price - l.discount) * l.qty), Qt::AlignRight);
        }
        t->setMinimumSize(660, 200);
        t->setStyleSheet("QTableWidget{border:1px solid #dce9ff;border-radius:8px;}");
        v->addWidget(t);
        // totals breakdown
        auto *sum = new QFormLayout;
        sum->setLabelAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft);
        auto right = [](const QString &x, bool big = false)
        {
            auto *l = new QLabel(x);
            l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignRight);
            if (big) l->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:15px;");
            return l;
        };
        sum->addRow("Subtotal", right(W::money(o.subtotal)));
        sum->addRow("Discount", right(o.discount > 0 ? W::money(-o.discount) : W::money(0)));
        sum->addRow("Tax", right(W::money(o.tax)));
        sum->addRow("Payment", right(o.payment.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : o.payment));
        auto *totalLbl = new QLabel("Total");
        totalLbl->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:15px;");
        sum->addRow(totalLbl, right(W::money(o.total), true));
        v->addLayout(sum);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        auto *bill = W::button("View Bill", "", "bills");
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(bill);
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        QObject::connect(bill, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         { receipt(&d, o); });
        d.exec();
    }

    void productDescription(QWidget *parent, int productId)
    {
        const ProductDescriptionView pd = AppState::instance().describe(productId);
        if (pd.title.isEmpty())
            return;
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Product description");
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(22, 18, 22, 16);
        v->setSpacing(10);
        auto *top = new QHBoxLayout;
        top->addWidget(W::label(pd.title, "h1"));
        const bool out = pd.level == "out", low = pd.level == "low";
        top->addWidget(W::badge(out ? "Out of stock" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock", out ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green"));
        top->addStretch();
        v->addLayout(top);
        auto *body = new QHBoxLayout;
        auto *form = new QFormLayout;
        form->setHorizontalSpacing(24);
        form->setVerticalSpacing(8);
        for (const auto &f : pd.fields)
            form->addRow(W::label(f.first.toUpper(), "caption"), W::label(f.second, "h2"));
        body->addLayout(form, 1);
        QPixmap px(pd.imagePath);
        if (!px.isNull())
        {
            auto *img = new QLabel;
            img->setPixmap(px.scaledToWidth(200, Qt::SmoothTransformation));
            body->addWidget(img, 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        }
        v->addLayout(body);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        d.exec();
    }

    void loadIssues(QWidget *parent)

        h->addStretch();
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        d.exec();
    }

    bool payment(QWidget *parent, double total, QString &mode, double &tendered, QString &merchant)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Instant Payment");
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(22, 20, 22, 18);
        v->setSpacing(12);
        v->addWidget(W::label("TOTAL PAYABLE", "caption"));
        auto *tot = W::label(W::money(total), "bigTotal");
        v->addWidget(tot);

        auto *modes = new QHBoxLayout;
        auto *group = new QButtonGroup(&d);
        const QStringList names = {"Cash", "Card", "Wallet"};
        const QStringList icons = {"cash", "card", "tag"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        {
            auto *b = W::button(names[i], "tab", icons[i]);
            b->setCheckable(true);
            b->setMinimumHeight(40);
            group->addButton(b, i);
            modes->addWidget(b);
        }
        group->button(0)->setChecked(true);
        v->addLayout(modes);

        auto *tenderRow = new QWidget;
        auto *th = new QHBoxLayout(tenderRow);
        th->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        th->addWidget(W::label("Cash tendered", "muted"));
        auto *spin = new QDoubleSpinBox;
        spin->setPrefix("PKR ");
        spin->setDecimals(2);
        spin->setRange(0, 10000000);
        spin->setValue(qCeil(total / 10.0) * 10.0);
        spin->setMinimumWidth(130);
        th->addStretch();
        th->addWidget(spin);
        v->addWidget(tenderRow);
        auto *change = W::label("", "h2");
        v->addWidget(change);
        auto *merchantRow = new QWidget;
        auto *mh = new QHBoxLayout(merchantRow);
        mh->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        auto *merchantName = W::label("Bank / wallet name", "muted");
        auto *merchantInput = new QLineEdit;
        merchantInput->setPlaceholderText("e.g. HBL, JazzCash");
        mh->addWidget(merchantName);
        mh->addWidget(merchantInput, 1);
        v->addWidget(merchantRow);

        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel");
        auto *ok = W::button("Confirm Payment", "primary", "check");
        h->addWidget(cancel);
        h->addWidget(ok);
        v->addLayout(h);

        auto refresh = [&]
        {
            const bool cash = group->checkedId() == 0;
            tenderRow->setVisible(cash);
            change->setVisible(cash);
            merchantRow->setVisible(!cash);
            const double c = spin->value() - total;
            change->setText(c >= -0.005 ? "Change due: " + W::money(c) : "Short by " + W::money(-c));
            change->setStyleSheet(c >= -0.005 ? "color:#006329;" : "color:#ba1a1a;");
            ok->setEnabled(cash ? c >= -0.005 : !merchantInput->text().trimmed().isEmpty());
        };
        QObject::connect(group, &QButtonGroup::idClicked, &d, [&](int)
                         { refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(spin, qOverload<double>(&QDoubleSpinBox::valueChanged), &d, [&](double)
                         { refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(merchantInput, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString&)
                         { refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);
        QObject::connect(ok, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        refresh();
        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;
        const int id = group->checkedId();
        mode = names[id];
        tendered = id == 0 ? spin->value() : 0;
        merchant = id == 0 ? QString() : merchantInput->text().trimmed();
        return true;
    }

    bool login(QWidget *parent, UserRole &role, QString &name)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Syntro-Check - Sign in");
        d.setMinimumWidth(420);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(28, 24, 28, 22);
        v->setSpacing(12);

        auto *title = new QLabel("Sign in to Syntro-Check");
        title->setStyleSheet("font-size:20px; font-weight:800; color:#1b2b55;");
        auto *sub = new QLabel("Enter your name and choose your role.");
        sub->setStyleSheet("color:#5b6478;");
        v->addWidget(title);
        v->addWidget(sub);

        auto *nameEdit = new QLineEdit;
        nameEdit->setPlaceholderText("Your name");
        nameEdit->setMinimumHeight(38);
        v->addWidget(nameEdit);

        // One button per role; the button pressed decides the role.
        auto *cashier = W::button("Cashier", "primary", "cart");
        auto *manager = W::button("Manager", "soft", "products");
        for (auto *b : {cashier, manager})
            b->setMinimumHeight(46);
        auto *row = new QHBoxLayout;
        row->setSpacing(10);
        row->addWidget(cashier);
        row->addWidget(manager);
        v->addLayout(row);

        auto *hint = new QLabel("Cashier: record orders and generate bills.\nManager: view products only.");
        hint->setStyleSheet("color:#8a94ab; font-size:11px;");
        v->addWidget(hint);

        QObject::connect(cashier, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&] { role = UserRole::Cashier; d.accept(); });
        QObject::connect(manager, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&] { role = UserRole::Manager; d.accept(); });
        QObject::connect(nameEdit, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, cashier, &QPushButton::click);

        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;
        name = nameEdit->text().trimmed();
        if (name.isEmpty())
            name = RolePolicy::name(role);
        return true;
    }
}

#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "DashboardPage.h"
#include "OtherPages.h"
#include "RecordOrderPage.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCloseEvent>

#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QMessageBox>

#include <QStackedWidget>
#include <QTimer>

namespace
{
    struct NavDef
    {
        const char *text, *icon, *key;
    };
    const NavDef kNav[] = {
        {"Dashboard", "dashboard", "F1"}, {"Record Order", "cart", "F2"}, {"Orders", "orders", "F3"},
        {"Products", "products", "F4"}, {"Barcode Scanner", "barcode", "F5"}, {"Bills", "bills", "F6"},
        {"Settings", "settings", "F10"}};
    const char *kSub[] = {"Today's performance at a glance", "Scan or search products and build the order",
                          "Every order from this shift", "Catalog and stock levels", "Scan a barcode to look up a product",
                          "Printable bills for paid orders", "Tax, discount and scanning preferences"};
}

MainWindow::MainWindow(UserRole role, const QString &userName, QWidget *parent)
    : QMainWindow(parent), m_role(role), m_userName(userName.isEmpty() ? RolePolicy::name(role) : userName)
{
    setWindowTitle("Syntro-Check");
    setMinimumSize(1100, 700);
    resize(1360, 860);

    auto *root = new QWidget;
    root->setObjectName("root");
    setCentralWidget(root);
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(root);
    h->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    h->setSpacing(0);
    h->addWidget(buildSidebar());

    auto *right = new QWidget;
    auto *rv = new QVBoxLayout(right);
    rv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    rv->setSpacing(0);
    rv->addWidget(buildTopBar());

    m_stack = new QStackedWidget;
    m_dashboard = new DashboardPage;
    m_record = new RecordOrderPage;
    m_orders = new OrdersPage;
    m_products = new ProductsPage;
    m_scanner = new ScannerPage;
    m_bills = new BillsPage;
    m_settings = new SettingsPage;
    for (QWidget *w : QList<QWidget *>{m_dashboard, m_record, m_orders, m_products, m_scanner, m_bills, m_settings})
        m_stack->addWidget(w);
    rv->addWidget(m_stack, 1);
    // Manager can only view products: no buttons that add to an order.
    const bool viewOnly = !RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, RecordOrder);
    m_products->setViewOnly(viewOnly);
    m_scanner->setViewOnly(viewOnly);
    h->addWidget(right, 1);

    m_toast = new QLabel(root);
    m_toast->setObjectName("toast");
    m_toast->hide();
    m_toastTimer = new QTimer(this);
    m_toastTimer->setSingleShot(true);
    connect(m_toastTimer, &QTimer::timeout, m_toast, &QWidget::hide);

    // page wiring
    for (QObject *o : QList<QObject *>{m_dashboard, m_record, m_orders, m_products, m_scanner, m_bills, m_settings})
        connect(o, SIGNAL(toast(QString)), this, SLOT(showToast(QString)));
    connect(m_dashboard, &DashboardPage::navigate, this, &MainWindow::goTo);
    connect(m_dashboard, &DashboardPage::lookup, this, [this](const QString &text)
            {
        if (text.trimmed().isEmpty()) return;
        if (AppState::instance().findByCode(text)) { m_products->setSearch(text); goTo(Products); }
        else { m_orders->setSearch(text); goTo(Orders); } });
    connect(m_products, &ProductsPage::addToOrder, this, [this](const QString &ean)
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->showCode(ean, true); });
    connect(m_scanner, &ScannerPage::sendToOrder, this, [this](const QString &ean)
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->showCode(ean, true); });

    // F-keys
    for (int n : {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10})
    {
        auto *sc = new QShortcut(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F1 + n - 1), this);
        sc->setContext(Qt::ApplicationShortcut);
        connect(sc, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this, n]
                { handleFKey(n); });
    }
    auto *k = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+K"), this);
    connect(k, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(Dashboard)) m_dashboard->focusLookup(); });
    auto *nd = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+N"), this);
    connect(nd, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->newDraft(); });

    goTo(RolePolicy::startPage(m_role));
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildSidebar()
{
    auto *sb = new QFrame;
    sb->setObjectName("sidebar");
    sb->setFixedWidth(232);
    auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(sb);
    v->setContentsMargins(14, 18, 14, 16);
    v->setSpacing(6);

    auto *brand = new QHBoxLayout;
    brand->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
    brand->setSpacing(11);
    auto *logo = new QFrame;
    logo->setObjectName("logoBox");
    logo->setFixedSize(42, 42);
    auto *ll = new QVBoxLayout(logo);
    ll->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    auto *ls = new QLabel("S");
    ls->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    ll->addWidget(ls);
    brand->addWidget(logo);
    auto *bt = new QVBoxLayout;
    bt->setSpacing(0);
    bt->addWidget(W::label("Syntro-Check", "brand")); // app name, white, in the sidebar
    brand->addLayout(bt);
    brand->addStretch();
    v->addLayout(brand);
    v->addSpacing(18);

    for (int i = 0; i < PageCount; ++i)
    {
        auto *b = new QPushButton;
        b->setObjectName("nav");
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        b->setFixedHeight(44);
        b->setToolTip(QString("%1  (%2)").arg(kNav[i].text, kNav[i].key));
        auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(b);
        bh->setContentsMargins(14, 0, 14, 0);
        bh->setSpacing(13);
        auto *ic = new QLabel;
        ic->setObjectName("navIcon");
        ic->setFixedSize(20, 20);
        ic->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(kNav[i].icon, QColor("#b8c3e0"), 20));
        auto *tx = new QLabel(kNav[i].text);
        for (QWidget *l : {static_cast<QWidget *>(ic), static_cast<QWidget *>(tx)})
            l->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents);
        bh->addWidget(ic);
        bh->addWidget(tx, 1);
        v->addWidget(b);
        m_nav << b;
        b->setVisible(RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, i)); // hide pages this role cannot use
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, i]
                { goTo(i); });
    }

    v->addStretch();
    auto *user = W::card(sb, "userCard");
    auto *uh = new QHBoxLayout(user);
    uh->setContentsMargins(10, 9, 10, 9);
    uh->setSpacing(10);
    auto *av = new QLabel(m_userName.left(1).toUpper());
    av->setObjectName("avatar");
    av->setFixedSize(34, 34);
    av->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    uh->addWidget(av);
    auto *un = new QVBoxLayout;
    un->setSpacing(0);
    un->addWidget(W::label(m_userName, "userName"));
    un->addWidget(W::label(RolePolicy::name(m_role), "userRole"));
    uh->addLayout(un, 1);
    v->addWidget(user);
    v->addSpacing(4);
    auto *logoutB = new QPushButton("  Log out");
    logoutB->setObjectName("exitBtn");
    logoutB->setIcon(Theme::icon("user", QColor("#c9d3ee"), 16));
    logoutB->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    v->addWidget(logoutB);
    v->addSpacing(4);
    connect(logoutB, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            { m_loggedOut = true; if (!close()) m_loggedOut = false; });
    auto *exitB = new QPushButton("  Exit");
    exitB->setObjectName("exitBtn");
    exitB->setIcon(Theme::icon("logout", QColor("#c9d3ee"), 16));
    exitB->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    v->addWidget(exitB);
    connect(exitB, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QWidget::close);
    return sb;
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildTopBar()
{
    auto *bar = new QFrame;
    bar->setObjectName("topbar");
    bar->setFixedHeight(72);
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(bar);
    h->setContentsMargins(24, 0, 20, 0);
    h->setSpacing(14);
    auto *t = new QVBoxLayout;
    t->setSpacing(0);
    m_title = W::label("", "pageTitle");
    m_subtitle = W::label("", "pageSub");
    t->addStretch();
    t->addWidget(m_title);
    t->addWidget(m_subtitle);
    t->addStretch();
    h->addLayout(t);
    h->addStretch();

    // Dark Mode Toggle Button (from dedicated DarkModeManager module)
    auto *darkToggle = barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance().createTopBarToggle(bar);
    h->addWidget(darkToggle, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);

    connect(&barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance(), &barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::themeChanged,
            this, [this](bool isDark) {
                showToast(isDark ? "Switched to Dark Mode" : "Switched to Light Mode");
                AppState::instance().emitChanged(); // rebuild tables so their text colours match the theme
            });

    auto *chip = new QFrame;
    chip->setObjectName("chip");
    auto *ch = new QHBoxLayout(chip);
    ch->setContentsMargins(14, 5, 14, 7);
    ch->setSpacing(10);
    ch->addWidget(W::iconLabel("clock", Theme::primary, 18));
    auto *cl = new QVBoxLayout;
    cl->setSpacing(0);
    m_clockTime = W::label("", "clockTime");
    m_clockDate = W::label("", "clockDate");
    cl->addWidget(m_clockTime);
    cl->addWidget(m_clockDate);
    ch->addLayout(cl);
    h->addWidget(chip, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    auto *timer = new QTimer(this);
    auto tick = [this]
    {
        const auto now = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
        m_clockTime->setText(now.toString("HH:mm:ss"));
        m_clockDate->setText(now.toString("ddd, d MMM yyyy"));
    };
    connect(timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, tick);
    timer->start(1000);
    tick();
    return bar;
}

bool MainWindow::goTo(int page)
{
    if (page < 0 || page >= PageCount)
        return false;
    if (!RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, page))
    {
        showToast(RolePolicy::name(m_role) + " can only view products.");
        return false;
    }
    m_stack->setCurrentIndex(page);
    m_title->setText(kNav[page].text);
    m_subtitle->setText(kSub[page]);
    for (int i = 0; i < m_nav.size(); ++i)
    {
        m_nav[i]->setProperty("active", i == page);
        if (auto *ic = m_nav[i]->findChild<QLabel *>("navIcon"))
            ic->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(kNav[i].icon, i == page ? QColor("white") : QColor("#b8c3e0"), 20));
        Theme::repolish(m_nav[i]);
        for (auto *c : m_nav[i]->findChildren<QLabel *>())
            Theme::repolish(c);
    }
    if (page == RecordOrder)
        m_record->focusInput();
    return true;
}

QPixmap MainWindow::grabPage(int page)
{
    goTo(page);
    QApplication::processEvents();
    return grab();
}

void MainWindow::handleFKey(int n)
{
    const bool onRecord = m_stack->currentIndex() == RecordOrder;
    switch (n)
    {
    case 1: goTo(Dashboard); break;
    case 2: goTo(RecordOrder); break;
    case 3: goTo(Orders); break;
    case 4:
        if (onRecord) m_record->quickLookup();
        else goTo(Products);
        break;
    case 5: goTo(Scanner); break;
    case 6: goTo(Bills); break;
    case 9:
        if (!onRecord && !goTo(RecordOrder)) break;
        m_record->simulateScan();
        break;
    case 10:
        if (onRecord) m_record->checkout();
        else goTo(Settings);
        break;
    }
}

void MainWindow::showToast(const QString &text)
{
    m_toast->setText(text);
    m_toast->adjustSize();
    positionToast();
    m_toast->raise();
    m_toast->show();
    m_toastTimer->start(2600);
}

void MainWindow::positionToast()
{
    if (!m_toast)
        return;
    const QWidget *root = centralWidget();
    m_toast->move((root->width() - m_toast->width()) / 2 + 116, root->height() - m_toast->height() - 36);
}

void MainWindow::resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *e)
{
    QMainWindow::resizeEvent(e);
    positionToast();
}

void MainWindow::closeEvent(QCloseEvent *e)
{
    const auto r = m_loggedOut
        ? QMessageBox::question(this, "Log out", "Log out and return to the sign-in screen? The current draft will be lost.")
        : QMessageBox::question(this, "Exit Terminal", "Close Syntro-Check? The current draft will be lost.");
    if (r == QMessageBox::Yes)
        e->accept();
    else
        e->ignore();
}

#pragma once

#include <QString>
#include <QKeySequence>
#include <QVector>

/**
 * Represents a single navigation entry in the sidebar.
 * Used by MainWindow to build the UI dynamically.
 */
struct NavItem {
    QString id;          // internal identifier (e.g. "dashboard")
    QString text;        // visible label
    QString icon;        // icon name used by Theme::iconPixmap
    QKeySequence shortcut; // optional hot‑key (F1, F2, …)
};

/**
 * NavigationModel supplies the list of pages for the POS UI.
 * It can later be backed by a JSON file or a plugin system – for now it
 * returns a static vector, making it OCP‑friendly.
 */
class NavigationModel {
public:
    static QVector<NavItem> items();
};
