#include "OtherPages.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QListWidget>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QTime>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace
{
    QTableWidget* makeTable(const QStringList& headers)
    {
        auto* t = new QTableWidget(0, headers.size());
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers);
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
        t->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::SingleSelection);
        t->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Stretch);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setHighlightSections(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        t->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
        return t;
    }
    QTableWidgetItem* item(const QString& s, Qt::Alignment a = Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter,
        const QString& color = "", bool bold = false)
    {
        auto* i = new QTableWidgetItem(s);
        i->setTextAlignment(a);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }
    QWidget* wrapW(QWidget* w, Qt::Alignment a = Qt::AlignLeft)
    {
        auto* c = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(6, 0, 6, 0);
        h->setAlignment(a | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        h->addWidget(w);
        return c;
    }
    QWidget* pageShell(QWidget* page, QVBoxLayout*& body)
    {
        body = W::pageLayout(page);
        return page;
    }
    [[maybe_unused]] QFrame* kpiCard(const QString& cap, const QString& icon, QLabel*& value)
    {
        auto* c = W::card(nullptr);
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 10);
        auto* v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(0);
        v->addWidget(W::label(cap, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        value->setStyleSheet("font-size:20px; font-weight:700;");
        v->addWidget(value);
        h->addLayout(v);
        h->addStretch();
        auto* ic = new QLabel;
        ic->setFixedSize(32, 32);
        ic->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        ic->setStyleSheet("background:#dce9ff; border-radius:6px;");
        ic->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, Theme::primary, 16));
        h->addWidget(ic);
        return c;
    }
    bool writeCsv(QWidget* parent, const QString& suggested, const QStringList& rows)
    {
        const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(parent, "Export CSV", suggested, "CSV (*.csv)");
        if (path.isEmpty())
            return false;
        QFile f(path);
        if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Text))
            return false;
        QTextStream out(&f);
        for (const auto& r : rows)
            out << r << '\n';
        return true;
    }
}

// =================================================================== OrdersPage
OrdersPage::OrdersPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);

    auto* card = W::card(this);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 10);
    v->setSpacing(10);

    auto* tools = new QHBoxLayout;
    tools->setSpacing(8);
    m_orderSearch = new QLineEdit;
    m_orderSearch->setPlaceholderText("Search by order number, customer or phone…");
    m_orderSearch->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_orderSearch->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    m_custSearch = new QLineEdit; // kept hidden: legacy field, not shown
    m_custSearch->hide();
    tools->addWidget(m_orderSearch, 3);
    tools->addStretch(1);
    auto* exp = W::button("Export CSV", "soft", "export");
    tools->addWidget(exp);
    v->addLayout(tools);

    auto* tabs = new QHBoxLayout;
    tabs->setSpacing(6);
    for (const QString& name : { "All", "Paid", "In Progress", "On Hold" })
    {
        auto* b = W::button(name, "tab");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        b->setChecked(name == "All");
        tabs->addWidget(b);
        m_tabs << b;
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, name]
            {
                m_filter = name;
                for (auto* t : m_tabs) t->setChecked(t->text() == name);
                rebuild(); });
    }
    tabs->addStretch();
    v->addLayout(tabs);

    m_table = makeTable({ "ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS SUMMARY", "PAYMENT", "AMOUNT", "STATUS", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    for (int c : {0, 1, 4, 5})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    for (int c : {2, 6, 7})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 175);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(6, 120);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(7, 150);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    m_showing = W::label("", "muted");
    v->addWidget(m_showing);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);

    connect(m_orderSearch, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(m_custSearch, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &OrdersPage::rebuild);
    connect(exp, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            QStringList rows{ "Order ID,Time,Customer,Contact,Items,Payment,Amount,Status" };
            for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
                rows << QString("%1,%2,\"%3\",\"%4\",%5,\"%6\",%7,%8").arg(o.id, o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"), o.customer, o.contact)
                .arg(o.itemCount()).arg(o.payment).arg(o.total, 0, 'f', 2).arg(o.status);
            if (writeCsv(this, "orders.csv", rows)) emit toast("Orders exported"); });
    rebuild();
}

void OrdersPage::setSearch(const QString& text)
{
    m_custSearch->clear();
    m_orderSearch->setText(text);
}

void OrdersPage::rebuild()
{
    const auto& all = AppState::instance().orders();
    QString q = m_orderSearch->text().trimmed();
    q.remove('#');
    QVector<OrderRecord> rows;
    int hold = 0;
    for (const auto& o : all)
    {
        if (o.status == "On Hold" || o.status == "In Progress")
            ++hold;
        if (m_filter != "All" && o.status != m_filter)
            continue;
        if (!q.isEmpty() && !o.id.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) && !o.customer.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
            !o.contact.contains(q))
            continue;
        rows << o;
    }
    auto& st = AppState::instance();
    (void)hold;
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& o = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 56);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item("#" + o.id, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm") + "\n" + o.shift));
        {
            auto* cw = new QWidget;
            auto* cl = new QVBoxLayout(cw);
            cl->setContentsMargins(8, 4, 8, 4);
            cl->setSpacing(0);
            auto* n = W::label(o.customer + (o.tier == "VIP" ? "  [VIP]" : ""));
            n->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
            auto* c = W::label(o.contact);
            c->setStyleSheet("color:#737686; font-size:11.5px;");
            cl->addWidget(n);
            cl->addWidget(c);
            m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, cw);
        }
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(o.summary()));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(o.payment));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(o.total), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 6, wrapW(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status))));
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        auto* view = W::button("", "flat", "eye");
        view->setToolTip("View details");
        h->addWidget(view);
        connect(view, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::orderDetails(this, copy); });
        auto* rc = W::button("", "flat", "bills");
        rc->setToolTip("Receipt");
        h->addWidget(rc);
        h->addStretch();
        connect(rc, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::receipt(this, copy); });
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 7, w);
    }
    m_showing->setText(QString("%1 of %2 orders").arg(rows.size()).arg(all.size()));
}

// =================================================================== ProductsPage
ProductsPage::ProductsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 10);
    v->setSpacing(10);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("products", Theme::primary, 20));
    h->addWidget(W::label("Product Catalog", "h1"));
    m_count = W::badge("", "blue");
    h->addWidget(m_count);
    h->addStretch();
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search name, acronym, id or barcode...");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(280);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    h->addWidget(m_search);
    v->addLayout(h);
    m_issues = W::button("", "soft", "warn");
    connect(m_issues, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { Dialogs::loadIssues(this); });
    h->insertWidget(3, m_issues);
    m_table = makeTable({ "ID", "ACRONYM", "BARCODE", "PRODUCT", "UNIT", "PRICE", "TAX", "DISC.", "STOCK", "STATUS", "" });
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    for (int c : {0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(10, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(10, 200);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &ProductsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::setSearch(const QString& text) { m_search->setText(text); }

void ProductsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    auto& st = AppState::instance();
    const QVector<ProductInfo> rows = q.isEmpty() ? st.products() : st.searchText(q);
    m_count->setText(QString("%1 products").arg(rows.size()));
    const int nIssues = st.productIssues().size();
    m_issues->setVisible(nIssues > 0);
    m_issues->setText(QString("Data issues (%1)").arg(nIssues));
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& p = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 44);
        const int limit = st.settings().lowStockThreshold;
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(QString::number(p.id)));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(p.sku, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(p.ean));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(p.name, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(p.price), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 6, item(W::money(p.tax), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        m_table->setItem(r, 7, item(W::money(p.discount), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        const bool low = p.stock <= limit;
        m_table->setItem(r, 8, item(QString::number(p.stock), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, low ? "#ba1a1a" : "", low));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 9, wrapW(W::badge(p.stock == 0 ? "Out" : low ? "Low" : "OK", p.stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green")));
        auto* d = W::button("Details", "soft");
        auto* cell = new QWidget;
        auto* cl = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        cl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 4, 0);
        cl->setSpacing(6);
        cl->addWidget(d);
        const QString ean = p.ean;
        const int pid = p.id;
        if (!m_viewOnly) // Manager views products only: no "Add" button
        {
            auto* b = W::button("Add", "mini");
            b->setEnabled(p.stock > 0);
            cl->addWidget(b);
            connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, ean]
                { emit addToOrder(ean); });
        }
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 10, cell);
        connect(d, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, pid]
            { Dialogs::productDescription(this, pid); });
    }
}

// =================================================================== BillsPage
BillsPage::BillsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 10);
    v->setSpacing(10);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("bills", Theme::primary, 20));
    h->addWidget(W::label("Bills", "h1"));
    h->addStretch();
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search bill or order ID...");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(280);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    h->addWidget(m_search);
    v->addLayout(h);
    m_table = makeTable({ "BILL NO.", "ORDER", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "TOTAL", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(6, 160);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &BillsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void BillsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    QVector<OrderRecord> rows;
    for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
        if (o.status == "Paid" && !o.billId.isEmpty() &&
            (q.isEmpty() || o.id.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.billId.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.customer.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive)))
            rows << o;
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& o = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 46);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(o.billId, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(o.id));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(o.customer));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(o.payment));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(o.total), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        auto* view = W::button("View Bill", "mini");
        h->addWidget(view);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 6, w);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        connect(view, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::receipt(this, copy); });
    }
}

// =================================================================== ScannerPage
ScannerPage::ScannerPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->setSpacing(12);

    auto* cam = W::card(this);
    auto* cv = new QVBoxLayout(cam);
    cv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    cv->setSpacing(10);
    W::headerRow(cv, "barcode", "Barcode Scanner");
    m_feed = new CameraFeedWidget;
    m_feed->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
    m_feed->setMinimumHeight(300);
    cv->addWidget(m_feed, 1);
    auto* btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_toggle = W::button("Start camera", "primary", "play");
    btns->addWidget(m_toggle);
    btns->addStretch();
    cv->addLayout(btns);
    auto* mr = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_manual = new QLineEdit;
    m_manual->setPlaceholderText("Or type an 8-digit EAN and press Enter");
    m_manual->setMaxLength(12);
    auto* go = W::button("Lookup", "soft", "search");
    mr->addWidget(m_manual, 1);
    mr->addWidget(go);
    cv->addLayout(mr);
    h->addWidget(cam, 6);

    auto* side = W::card(this);
    auto* sv = new QVBoxLayout(side);
    sv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    sv->setSpacing(10);
    W::headerRow(sv, "clock", "Recent Scans");
    m_last = W::label("No scans yet", "h2");
    sv->addWidget(m_last);
    m_history = new QListWidget;
    m_history->setStyleSheet("QListWidget{border:1px solid #dbe4f7;border-radius:10px;padding:4px;} QListWidget::item{padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid #e8eefb;}");
    sv->addWidget(m_history, 1);
    m_describe = W::button("Show description", "soft", "search");
    m_describe->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_describe);
    m_send = W::button("Add last scan to order", "primary", "cart");
    m_send->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_send);
    h->addWidget(side, 4);
    root->addLayout(h, 1);

    connect(m_toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            const bool on = !m_feed->running();
            m_feed->setRunning(on);
            const bool running = m_feed->running();
            m_toggle->setText(running ? "Stop camera" : "Start camera");
            m_toggle->setIcon(Theme::icon(running ? "stop" : "play", QColor("white"), 16)); });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, &ScannerPage::toast);
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString& code) {
        const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
        if (product) { m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  " + product->name, true); show(*product); }
        else { m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  not in Product.txt", false); emit toast("Barcode " + code + " not found in Product.txt"); }
        });
    auto lookup = [this]
        {
            const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(m_manual->text());
            if (!p)
            {
                emit toast("No product found for \"" + m_manual->text() + "\"");
                return;
            }
            show(*p);
            m_manual->clear();
        };
    connect(go, &QPushButton::clicked, this, lookup);
    connect(m_manual, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, lookup);
    connect(m_send, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { emit sendToOrder(m_lastEan); });
    connect(m_describe, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_lastId); });
}

void ScannerPage::scanNext()
{
    if (!m_feed->running()) m_feed->setRunning(true);
    if (!m_feed->running()) emit toast("Unable to start camera scanning");
    else {
        m_toggle->setText("Stop camera");
        m_toggle->setIcon(Theme::icon("stop", QColor("white"), 16));
    }
}

void ScannerPage::show(const ProductInfo& p)
{
    m_feed->setCode(p.ean);
    m_lastEan = p.ean;
    m_lastId = p.id;
    m_last->setText(p.name + " • " + W::money(p.price));
    m_history->insertItem(0, QString("%1   %2   %3").arg(QTime::currentTime().toString("HH:mm:ss"), p.ean, p.name));
    m_send->setEnabled(true);
    m_describe->setEnabled(true);
    // Show description only on request; opening a modal on every camera frame is disruptive.
}

// =================================================================== SettingsPage
SettingsPage::SettingsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    auto* card = W::card(this);
    card->setMaximumWidth(620);
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 16);
    v->setSpacing(14);
    W::headerRow(v, "settings", "Terminal Settings");
    auto& s = AppState::instance().settings();

    auto* form = new QFormLayout;
    form->setHorizontalSpacing(20);
    form->setVerticalSpacing(12);
    auto* low = new QSpinBox;
    low->setRange(0, 1000);
    low->setSuffix(" units");
    low->setValue(s.lowStockThreshold);
    auto* autoAdd = new QCheckBox("Add scanned items to the order automatically");
    autoAdd->setChecked(s.autoAddScans);
    auto* beep = new QCheckBox("Play a beep on successful scan");
    beep->setChecked(s.beepOnScan);
    form->addRow("Low-stock limit", low);
    form->addRow("Scanning", autoAdd);
    form->addRow("", beep);
    v->addLayout(form);
    v->addWidget(W::label("Tax and discount come per product from Product.txt. Use the Dark Mode button at the top to switch themes.", "muted"));
    v->addStretch();
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addStretch();
    auto* save = W::button("Apply", "primary", "check");
    h->addWidget(save);
    v->addLayout(h);
    connect(save, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [=, this]
        {
            auto& st = AppState::instance().settings();
            st.lowStockThreshold = low->value();
            AppState::instance().emitChanged();
            st.autoAddScans = autoAdd->isChecked();
            st.beepOnScan = beep->isChecked();
            emit toast("Settings applied"); });
    root->addWidget(card);
    root->addStretch();
}
void ProductsPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_viewOnly = on;
    m_table->setRowCount(0); // drop the old rows (with their buttons) before rebuilding
    rebuild();
}

void ScannerPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_send->setVisible(!on);
}

#include "RecordOrderPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QInputDialog>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QtMath>

namespace
{
    QWidget* twoLine(const QString& top, const QString& bottom)
    {
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(w);
        v->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4);
        v->setSpacing(1);
        auto* t = W::label(top);
        t->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700; font-size:13.5px;");
        t->setWordWrap(true);
        auto* b = W::label(bottom);
        b->setStyleSheet("color:#8a94ab; font-size:11.5px;");
        v->addWidget(t);
        v->addWidget(b);
        return w;
    }
    QTableWidgetItem* cell(const QString& text, const QString& color = "", bool bold = false)
    {
        auto* i = new QTableWidgetItem(text);
        i->setTextAlignment(Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }
}

RecordOrderPage::RecordOrderPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    auto* root = W::pageLayout(this);
    root->addWidget(buildCustomerStrip());
    auto* body = new QHBoxLayout;
    body->setSpacing(0);
    body->addWidget(buildLeft(), 36);
    body->addWidget(buildRight(), 64);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);

    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::scanned, this, [this](const QString& ean)
        { showCode(ean, true); });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString& ean) {
        const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(ean);
        if (product) m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  " + product->name, true);
        else m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  not in Product.txt", false);
        AppState::instance().emitScan(ean);
        });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, &RecordOrderPage::toast);
    loadDemoDraft();
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildCustomerStrip()
{
    auto* strip = W::card(this, "strip");
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(strip);
    h->setContentsMargins(14, 8, 14, 8);
    h->setSpacing(12);
    h->addWidget(W::iconTile("user", Qt::white, "#7fb0ff", "#2b5fe8", 40));

    auto field = [&](const QString& cap, QWidget* content, int stretch)
        {
            auto* f = new QFrame;
            f->setObjectName("field");
            auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(f);
            v->setContentsMargins(12, 5, 12, 6);
            v->setSpacing(0);
            v->addWidget(W::label(cap, "caption"));
            v->addWidget(content);
            h->addWidget(f, stretch);
        };
    m_customer = new QLineEdit("Marcus Vance");
    m_customer->setProperty("flat", true);
    m_customer->setPlaceholderText("Walk-in customer");
    field("CUSTOMER", m_customer, 3);
    m_contact = new QLineEdit("+1 (555) 019-2834");
    m_contact->setProperty("flat", true);
    m_contact->setPlaceholderText("Phone (optional)");
    field("PHONE", m_contact, 3);


    h->addStretch(1);
    auto* sv = new QVBoxLayout;
    sv->setSpacing(0);
    sv->addWidget(W::label("ORDER", "caption"));
    m_sessionId = W::label("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId(), "mono");
    m_sessionId->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
    sv->addWidget(m_sessionId);
    h->addLayout(sv);
    return strip;
}

    m_pMeta = W::label("Scan a barcode or type one above.", "muted");

    pv->addWidget(info);

    m_addBtn->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(false);

    m_descBtn->setEnabled(false);
    pv->addWidget(m_descBtn);
    pv->addWidget(m_addBtn);
    connect(m_descBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { if (m_preview && m_preview->id > 0) Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_preview->id); });
    connect(m_addBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { if (m_preview) addToCart(*m_preview); });
    v->addWidget(pc, 3);
    return col;
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildRight()
{
    auto* col = new QWidget;
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(col);
    v->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    v->setSpacing(0);

    auto* oc = W::card(col);
    auto* ov = new QVBoxLayout(oc);
    ov->setContentsMargins(0, 14, 0, 10);
    ov->setSpacing(6);

    auto* head = new QHBoxLayout;
    head->setContentsMargins(18, 0, 14, 0);
    head->setSpacing(10);
    head->addWidget(W::iconLabel("orders", Theme::primary, 20));
    head->addWidget(W::label("Current order", "h1"));
    m_itemsBadge = W::badge("0 items", "grey");
    head->addWidget(m_itemsBadge);
    head->addStretch();
    auto* clear = W::button("Clear", "flat", "trash");
    head->addWidget(clear);
    ov->addLayout(head);
    connect(clear, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::newDraft);

    m_table = new QTableWidget(0, 6);
    m_table->setHorizontalHeaderLabels({ "PRODUCT", "PRICE", "QTY", "DISCOUNT", "TOTAL", "" });
    m_table->verticalHeader()->hide();
    m_table->setShowGrid(false);
    m_table->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::NoSelection);
    m_table->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
    m_table->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
    m_table->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    auto* hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Fixed);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    hh->setHighlightSections(false);
    hh->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(1, 78);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 112);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(3, 92);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(4, 96);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(5, 52);
    m_table->setWordWrap(false);
    ov->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    v->addWidget(oc, 1);

    auto* tc = W::card(col, "totals");
    auto* th = new QHBoxLayout(tc);
    th->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14);
    th->setSpacing(18);
    auto* grid = new QVBoxLayout;
    grid->setSpacing(8);
    auto row = [&](const QString& k, QLabel*& val)
        {
            auto* r = new QHBoxLayout;
            r->addWidget(W::label(k, "muted"));
            r->addStretch();
            val = W::label("PKR 0.00");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:14px;");
            r->addWidget(val);
            grid->addLayout(r);
        };
    row("Subtotal", m_gross);
    row("Discount", m_disc);
    row("Tax", m_tax);
    grid->addStretch();
    th->addLayout(grid, 1);

    auto* tb = W::card(tc, "totalBox");
    auto* tv = new QVBoxLayout(tb);
    tv->setContentsMargins(18, 12, 18, 14);
    tv->setSpacing(2);
    auto* cap = W::label("TOTAL", "caption");
    tv->addWidget(cap);
    m_total = W::label("PKR 0.00");
    m_total->setStyleSheet("font-size:34px; font-weight:800; color:white;");
    tv->addWidget(m_total);
    m_checkout = W::button("Checkout  [F10]", "primary", "check");
    m_checkout->setMinimumHeight(44);
    tv->addWidget(m_checkout);
    th->addWidget(tb, 1);
    connect(m_checkout, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::checkout);
    v->addWidget(tc);
    return col;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- behaviour
void RecordOrderPage::focusInput()
{
    m_manual->setFocus();
    m_manual->selectAll();
}

void RecordOrderPage::simulateScan()
{
    if (!m_feed->running()) m_feed->setRunning(true);
    if (!m_feed->running()) emit toast("Unable to start camera scanning");
    else {
        m_camToggle->setText("Stop camera");
        m_camToggle->setIcon(Theme::icon("stop", QColor("white"), 16));
    }
}

void RecordOrderPage::quickLookup()
{
    QStringList items;
    for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
        items << QString("%1  -  %2").arg(p.ean, p.name);
    bool ok = false;
    const QString pick = QInputDialog::getItem(this, "Browse products", "Choose a product:", items, 0, false, &ok);
    if (ok)
        showCode(pick.section("  -  ", 0, 0), false);
}

void RecordOrderPage::showCode(const QString& codeIn, bool fromScanner)
{
    const QString code = codeIn.trimmed();
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Enter a barcode first");
        return;
    }
    const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
    if (!p)
    {
        setPreview(std::nullopt);
        emit toast("No product found for \"" + code + "\"");
        return;
    }
    m_feed->setCode(p->ean);
    setPreview(p);
    m_manual->clear();
    if (fromScanner && AppState::instance().settings().autoAddScans)
        addToCart(*p);
}

void RecordOrderPage::setPreview(const std::optional<ProductInfo>& p)
{
    m_preview = p;
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->stock > 0);
    m_descBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->id > 0);
    if (!p)
    {
        m_pName->setText("Nothing scanned yet");
        m_pPrice->clear();
        m_pMeta->setText("Scan a barcode or type one above.");
        m_pStock->clear();
        W::setBadge(m_pBadge, "—", "grey");
        m_pThumb->clear();
        return;
    }
    m_pName->setText(p->name);
    m_pPrice->setText(W::money(p->price));
    m_pMeta->setText(QString("%1  •  %2  •  tax %3  •  disc %4").arg(p->ean, p->unit, W::money(p->tax), W::money(p->discount)));
    const bool low = p->stock <= AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
    m_pStock->setText(QString("%1 in stock").arg(p->stock));
    m_pStock->setStyleSheet(QString("font-weight:700; color:%1;").arg(low ? "#b3261e" : "#0b7a36"));
    W::setBadge(m_pBadge, p->stock == 0 ? "Out of stock" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock",
        p->stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green");

    // little 3D "box" thumbnail
    QPixmap pm(120, 144);
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(2);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter pa(&pm);
    pa.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    pa.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    pa.setBrush(QColor(20, 40, 110, 40));
    pa.drawEllipse(QRectF(6, 60, 46, 8));
    QLinearGradient fg(10, 8, 10, 62);
    fg.setColorAt(0, QColor("#7fb0ff"));
    fg.setColorAt(1, QColor("#2b5fe8"));
    pa.setBrush(fg);
    pa.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(8, 14, 34, 46), 4, 4);
    pa.setBrush(QColor("#1a3fae"));
    pa.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({ {42, 14}, {50, 8}, {50, 54}, {42, 60} }));
    pa.setBrush(QColor("#a9ccff"));
    pa.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({ {8, 14}, {16, 8}, {50, 8}, {42, 14} }));
    pa.setBrush(QColor(255, 255, 255, 230));
    pa.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(13, 26, 24, 22), 3, 3);
    pa.setPen(QColor("#1a3fae"));
    QFont f = pa.font();
    f.setPixelSize(8);
    f.setBold(true);
    pa.setFont(f);
    pa.drawText(QRectF(13, 26, 24, 22), Qt::AlignCenter, p->name.section(' ', 0, 0).left(5).toUpper());
    pa.end();
    m_pThumb->setPixmap(pm);
}

void RecordOrderPage::addToCart(const ProductInfo& p, int qty)
{
    int have = 0;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
            have = l.qty;
    const QString problem = AppState::instance().stockProblem(p.id, have, qty);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return;
    }
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
        {
            l.qty += qty;
            found = true;
        }
    if (!found)
        m_cart.push_back({ p, qty });
    rebuildTable();
    emit toast(QString("Added %1 × %2").arg(qty).arg(p.name));
}

void RecordOrderPage::changeQty(int row, int delta)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    auto& l = m_cart[row];
    const int nq = l.qty + delta;
    if (nq < 1)
    {
        removeRow(row);
        return;
    }
    const QString problem = AppState::instance().stockProblem(l.p.id, l.qty, delta);
    if (delta > 0 && !problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return;
    }
    l.qty = nq;
    rebuildTable();
}

void RecordOrderPage::removeRow(int row)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    m_cart.removeAt(row);
    rebuildTable();
}

void RecordOrderPage::rebuildTable()
{
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(m_cart.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < m_cart.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& l = m_cart[r];
        const double gross = l.p.price * l.qty;
        const double disc = l.p.discount * l.qty;
        const double tax = l.p.tax * l.qty;
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 62);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 0, twoLine(l.p.name, l.p.sku + "  •  " + l.p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, cell(W::money(l.p.price), "", true));
        auto* stp = new QtyStepper(l.qty);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, stp);
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::minus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, -1); });
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::plus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, +1); });
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, cell(disc > 0 ? W::money(-disc) : "—", disc > 0 ? "#0b7a36" : "#8a94ab"));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, cell(W::money(gross - disc + tax), "#2554d8", true));
        auto* del = new QPushButton;
        del->setProperty("kind", "flat");
        del->setIcon(Theme::icon("trash", Theme::error, 18));
        del->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        del->setToolTip("Remove");
        auto* wrap = new QWidget;
        auto* wl = new QHBoxLayout(wrap);
        wl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        wl->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        wl->addWidget(del);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, wrap);
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, r]
            { removeRow(r); });
    }
    updateTotals();
}

void RecordOrderPage::updateTotals()
{
    double gross = 0, disc = 0, tax = 0;
    int items = 0;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
    {
        gross += l.p.price * l.qty;
        disc += l.p.discount * l.qty;
        tax += l.p.tax * l.qty;
        items += l.qty;
    }
    const double total = gross - disc + tax;
    W::setBadge(m_itemsBadge, QString("%1 item%2").arg(items).arg(items == 1 ? "" : "s"), items ? "blue" : "grey");
    m_gross->setText(W::money(gross));
    m_disc->setText(disc > 0 ? W::money(-disc) : "PKR 0.00");
    m_tax->setText(W::money(tax));
    m_total->setText(W::money(total));
    m_checkout->setEnabled(items > 0);
}

void RecordOrderPage::loadDemoDraft()
{
    // Start with an empty order; products come from Product.txt.
    rebuildTable();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
}

void RecordOrderPage::newDraft()
{
    m_cart.clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    emit toast("New order started");
}

void RecordOrderPage::checkout()
{
    if (m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("The order is empty");
        return;
    }
    OrderRecord o;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        o.lines.push_back({ l.p.name, l.p.sku, l.qty, l.p.price, l.p.tax, l.p.discount, l.p.id });
    o.recompute();

    QString mode, merchant;
    double tendered = 0;
    if (!Dialogs::payment(this, o.total, mode, tendered, merchant))
        return;

    o.customer = m_customer->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : m_customer->text().trimmed();
    o.contact = m_contact->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "-" : m_contact->text().trimmed();
    o.payment = mode;
    o.merchant = merchant;
    o.tendered = tendered;
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.shift = "POS #04 • Shift A";
    QString checkoutError;
    if (!AppState::instance().addOrder(o, &checkoutError))
    {
        emit toast(checkoutError);
        return;
    }

    Dialogs::receipt(this, o);
    m_cart.clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    emit toast("Order " + o.id + " paid - " + W::money(o.total));
}

#include "Theme.h"
#include <QPainter>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QStyle>
#include <QWidget>

void Theme::repolish(QWidget *w)
{
    w->style()->unpolish(w);
    w->style()->polish(w);
    w->update();
}

QIcon Theme::icon(const QString &name, const QColor &color, int px)
{
    return QIcon(iconPixmap(name, color, px));
}

QPixmap Theme::iconPixmap(const QString &n, const QColor &c, int px)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(px * dpr), int(px * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(px / 24.0, px / 24.0);
    p.setPen(QPen(c, 1.9, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin));
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);

    auto line = [&](qreal x1, qreal y1, qreal x2, qreal y2)
    { p.drawLine(QPointF(x1, y1), QPointF(x2, y2)); };
    auto rect = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal w, qreal h, qreal r = 2)
    { p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(x, y, w, h), r, r); };
    auto circle = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal r)
    { p.drawEllipse(QPointF(x, y), r, r); };
    auto fillShape = [&](auto fn)
    {
        p.save();
        p.setBrush(c);
        fn();
        p.restore();
    };

    if (n == "dashboard")
    {
        rect(3, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(3, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
    }
    else if (n == "cart")
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(2, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(5, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(7.2, 15);
        path.lineTo(18.5, 15);
        path.lineTo(20.5, 7);
        path.lineTo(5.6, 7);
        p.drawPath(path);
        circle(9.5, 19.3, 1.5);
        circle(16.5, 19.3, 1.5);
    }
    else if (n == "orders")
    {
        rect(5, 3, 14, 18, 2);
        line(8.5, 8, 15.5, 8);
        line(8.5, 12, 15.5, 12);
        line(8.5, 16, 12.5, 16);
    }
    else if (n == "products")
    {
        rect(3, 4, 18, 5, 1.5);
        rect(4.5, 9, 15, 11, 1.5);
        line(10, 13, 14, 13);
    }
    else if (n == "barcode")
    {
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(c);
        const qreal xs[] = {3, 5.5, 8, 10, 13, 15, 17.5, 20};
        const qreal ws[] = {1.4, 0.9, 1.8, 0.9, 1.4, 1.8, 0.9, 1.2};
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
            p.drawRect(QRectF(xs[i], 4, ws[i], 16));
    }
    else if (n == "bills")
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(6, 3);
        path.lineTo(14, 3);
        path.lineTo(19, 8);
        path.lineTo(19, 21);
        path.lineTo(6, 21);
        path.closeSubpath();
        p.drawPath(path);
        line(14, 3, 14, 8);
        line(14, 8, 19, 8);
        line(9, 13, 16, 13);
        line(9, 17, 14, 17);
    }
    else if (n == "settings")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 3);
        circle(12, 12, 7);
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
        {
            p.save();
            p.translate(12, 12);
            p.rotate(i * 45);
            line(0, -7, 0, -10);
            p.restore();
        }
    }
    else if (n == "search")
    {
        circle(10.5, 10.5, 6);
        line(15, 15, 20.5, 20.5);
    }
    else if (n == "trash")
    {
        line(4, 6, 20, 6);
        line(9.5, 3.5, 14.5, 3.5);
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(6, 6);
        path.lineTo(7, 20);
        path.lineTo(17, 20);
        path.lineTo(18, 6);
        p.drawPath(path);
        line(10, 10, 10, 16);
        line(14, 10, 14, 16);
    }
    else if (n == "plus")
    {
        line(12, 5, 12, 19);
        line(5, 12, 19, 12);
    }
    else if (n == "minus")
    {
        line(5, 12, 19, 12);
    }
    else if (n == "play")
    {
        fillShape([&]
                  { p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{7, 4.5}, {19, 12}, {7, 19.5}})); });
    }
    else if (n == "pause")
    {
        line(9, 5, 9, 19);
        line(15, 5, 15, 19);
    }
    else if (n == "stop")
    {
        rect(6, 6, 12, 12, 1.5);
    }
    else if (n == "check")
    {
        circle(12, 12, 9);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{7.8, 12.3}, {10.7, 15.2}, {16.2, 9.2}}));
    }
    else if (n == "warn")
    {
        p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{12, 3.5}, {21.5, 20}, {2.5, 20}}));
        line(12, 9.5, 12, 14);
        line(12, 17, 12, 17.2);
    }
    else if (n == "lock")
    {
        rect(5, 11, 14, 10, 2);
        p.drawArc(QRectF(8, 3, 8, 12), 0, 180 * 16);
    }
    else if (n == "wifi")
    {
        p.drawArc(QRectF(2, 4, 20, 20), 50 * 16, 80 * 16);
        p.drawArc(QRectF(6, 8, 12, 12), 45 * 16, 90 * 16);
        fillShape([&]
                  { circle(12, 18.5, 1.3); });
    }
    else if (n == "clock")

        circle(12, 12, 9);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{12, 7}, {12, 12}, {15.5, 14}}));
    }
    else if (n == "user")

        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{4.5, 15}, {4.5, 20}, {19.5, 20}, {19.5, 15}}));
    }
    else if (n == "refresh")
    {

    }
    else if (n == "camera")
    {

    }
    else if (n == "info")

        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{10, 4}, {4, 4}, {4, 20}, {10, 20}}));
        line(9, 12, 20, 12);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{16, 8}, {20, 12}, {16, 16}}));
    }
    else if (n == "card")
    {
        rect(2.5, 5, 19, 14, 2.5);
        line(2.5, 10, 21.5, 10);
        line(6, 15, 10, 15);
    }
    else if (n == "cash")
    {
        rect(2.5, 6, 19, 12, 2);
        circle(12, 12, 2.8);
        line(5.5, 12, 5.6, 12);
        line(18.4, 12, 18.5, 12);
    }
    else if (n == "arrow-right")
    {
        line(5, 12, 19, 12);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{13, 6}, {19, 12}, {13, 18}}));
    }
    else if (n == "arrow-left")
    {
        line(19, 12, 5, 12);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{11, 6}, {5, 12}, {11, 18}}));
    }
    else if (n == "chevron-down")
    {
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{6, 9}, {12, 15}, {18, 9}}));
    }
    return QPixmap(pm);
}

namespace
{
    int g_dark = -1;
}

bool Theme::isDark()
{
    if (g_dark < 0)
        g_dark = QSettings("BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS").value("darkMode", false).toBool() ? 1 : 0;
    return g_dark == 1;
}

void Theme::setDark(bool on)
{
    g_dark = on ? 1 : 0;
    QSettings("BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS").setValue("darkMode", on);
}

QColor Theme::readable(const QColor &c)
{
    // In dark mode a dark colour (e.g. dark blue text) would vanish on the dark cards:
    // keep its hue but raise its lightness.
    if (!isDark() || c.lightnessF() >= 0.6)
        return c;
    return QColor::fromHslF(c.hslHueF() < 0 ? 0 : c.hslHueF(), c.hslSaturationF(), 0.72);
}

QString Theme::styleSheet()
{
    QString s = lightSheet();
    if (!isDark())
        return s;
    // Dark mode: swap the light palette for dark equivalents (order matters: specific first).
    const char *map[][2] = {
        {"#0f1b33", "#e6ecfb"}, {"#1b2a4d", "#dbe4fa"}, {"#f4f7fe", "#141b2e"}, {"#e8eefb", "#0f1524"},
        {"#eaf0fc", "#0f1524"}, {"#f3f6fe", "#18213a"}, {"#eef3ff", "#1d2742"}, {"#f6f9ff", "#1a2340"},
        {"#edf2fd", "#161e36"}, {"#f1f5fd", "#141b2e"}, {"#eef3fe", "#1d2742"}, {"#e4ecfd", "#26324f"},
        {"#e3eafb", "#26324f"}, {"#f3f6fc", "#1a2238"}, {"#eaf1ff", "#22304f"}, {"#d9e6ff", "#1b2845"},
        {"#e6eefe", "#26324f"}, {"#e0eaff", "#22304f"}, {"#e5eeff", "#22304f"}, {"#ffffff", "#1c2540"},
        {"white", "#1c2540"}, {"#dbe4f7", "#2a3556"}, {"#d3ddf3", "#2f3b5e"}, {"#d9e3f8", "#2f3b5e"},
        {"#dfe7f8", "#2a3556"}, {"#cbd8f2", "#26324f"}, {"#bcc9e8", "#232d4a"}, {"#c3d0ee", "#2f3b5e"},
        {"#c9d8fb", "#2f3b5e"}, {"#aebfee", "#232d4a"}, {"#b9c9ee", "#232d4a"}, {"#c6d3ee", "#232d4a"},
        {"#e8eefb", "#243050"}, {"#64708a", "#9aa7c7"}, {"#7a86a0", "#8795b8"}, {"#55617c", "#a9b6d6"},
        {"#1d46b4", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#004ac6", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#2554d8", "#8fb2ff"}, {"#dce9ff", "#2a3556"},
        {"#c3cfea", "#3a4770"}, {"#a3b3d8", "#4a5a8a"}, {"#a3adc5", "#5d6a8f"}, {"#8b96ad", "#6a7799"}};
    s.replace(QRegularExpression("color:\\s*(#ffffff|white)"), "color: @KEEPWHITE@");
    for (const auto &m : map)
        s.replace(QString::fromLatin1(m[0]), QString::fromLatin1(m[1]));
    s.replace("@KEEPWHITE@", "#ffffff");
    return s;
}

// Short "scanner" beep after a successful scan (if enabled in Settings).
// Windows Beep() blocks while it plays, so it runs on its own short-lived thread.
static void scanBeep()
{
    if (!AppState::instance().settings().beepOnScan)
        return;
#ifdef Q_OS_WIN
    std::thread([] { Beep(1800, 120); }).detach(); // 1800 Hz for 120 ms
#else
    QApplication::beep();
#endif
}
#include <QLinearGradient>
#include <QLocale>

#include <QPainterPath>

namespace W
{
    QFrame *card(QWidget *parent, const char *objectName)
    {
        const QString n = objectName;
        if (n == "card" || n == "strip" || n == "totals")
            return new Card(parent, false);
        if (n == "cardAlert")
            return new Card(parent, true);
        auto *f = new QFrame(parent);
        f->setObjectName(objectName);
        return f;
    }

    QVBoxLayout *pageLayout(QWidget *page)
    {
        page->setObjectName("page");
        auto *l = new QVBoxLayout(page);
        l->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 6);
        l->setSpacing(0);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *iconTile(const QString &icon, const QColor &fg, const QString &top, const QString &bottom, int size)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel;
        l->setFixedSize(size, size);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        l->setStyleSheet(QString("background: qlineargradient(x1:0,y1:0,x2:0,y2:1, stop:0 %1, stop:1 %2);"
                                 "border-radius:%3px; border-bottom:3px solid rgba(0,0,0,18%);")
                             .arg(top, bottom)
                             .arg(size / 3));
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, fg, size / 2));
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *label(const QString &text, const char *objectName, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(text, parent);
        if (objectName)
            l->setObjectName(objectName);
        return l;
    }

    void setBadge(QLabel *l, const QString &text, const QString &kind)
    {
        l->setText(text);
        l->setProperty("badge", kind);
        Theme::repolish(l);
    }

    QLabel *badge(const QString &text, const QString &kind, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        setBadge(l, text, kind);
        return l;
    }

    QPushButton *button(const QString &text, const QString &kind, const QString &icon, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *b = new QPushButton(text, parent);
        if (!kind.isEmpty())
            b->setProperty("kind", kind);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
        {
            const bool light = kind == "primary";
            const bool danger = kind == "danger";
            const QColor c = light ? QColor("white") : danger ? Theme::error
                                                              : Theme::primary;
            b->setIcon(Theme::icon(icon, c, 16));
            b->setIconSize({16, 16});
        }
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        return b;
    }

    QLabel *iconLabel(const QString &icon, const QColor &color, int px, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, color, px));
        l->setFixedSize(px, px);
        l->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *dot(const QColor &color, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setFixedSize(8, 8);
        l->setStyleSheet(QString("background:%1; border-radius:4px;").arg(color.name()));
        return l;
    }

    QFrame *hline(QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *f = new QFrame(parent);
        f->setFixedHeight(1);
        f->setStyleSheet("background:#dce9ff; border:none;");
        return f;
    }

    QString money(double v, bool sign)
    {
        const QString s = QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(qAbs(v), 'f', 2);
        if (v < 0)
            return "-PKR " + s;
        return (sign ? "+PKR " : "PKR ") + s;
    }

    QString statusBadgeKind(const QString &status)
    {
        if (status == "Paid")
            return "green";
        if (status == "In Progress")
            return "blue";
        if (status == "On Hold")
            return "amber";
        return "grey";
    }

    QHBoxLayout *headerRow(QVBoxLayout *into, const QString &icon, const QString &title, QWidget *right)
    {
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->setSpacing(8);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
            h->addWidget(iconLabel(icon, Theme::primary, 18));
        h->addWidget(label(title, "h1"));
        h->addStretch();
        if (right)
            h->addWidget(right, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        into->addLayout(h);
        return h;
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Card
Card::Card(QWidget *parent, bool alert) : QFrame(parent), m_alert(alert)
{
    setContentsMargins(8, 4, 8, 10);
    setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, false);
}

void Card::resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *e)
{
    QFrame::resizeEvent(e);
    const qreal dpr = devicePixelRatioF();
    m_shadow = QPixmap(QSize(width(), height()) * dpr);
    m_shadow.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    m_shadow.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter p(&m_shadow);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    const QRectF body = QRectF(rect()).adjusted(8, 4, -8, -10);
    for (int i = 9; i >= 0; --i)
    {
        p.setBrush(QColor(25, 50, 120, 5 + (9 - i) / 3));
        p.drawRoundedRect(body.translated(0, 4).adjusted(-i, -i + 1, i, i + 1), 14 + i, 14 + i);
    }
}

void Card::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.drawPixmap(0, 0, m_shadow);
    const QRectF body = QRectF(rect()).adjusted(8, 4, -8, -10);
    const bool dark = Theme::isDark(); // card colours follow light / dark mode
    QLinearGradient g(body.topLeft(), body.bottomLeft());
    if (dark)
    {
        g.setColorAt(0, m_alert ? QColor("#3a2230") : QColor("#1f2a47"));
        g.setColorAt(1, m_alert ? QColor("#2e1c28") : QColor("#1a233d"));
    }
    else
    {
        g.setColorAt(0, QColor("#ffffff"));
        g.setColorAt(1, m_alert ? QColor("#fff2f0") : QColor("#f4f7ff"));
    }
    p.setBrush(g);
    p.setPen(QPen(m_alert ? QColor(255, 140, 130, 190) : (dark ? QColor(90, 120, 200, 70) : QColor(40, 70, 150, 34)), 1));
    p.drawRoundedRect(body.adjusted(.5, .5, -.5, -.5), 14, 14);
    // lit top edge for the "raised" look
    p.setPen(QPen(dark ? QColor(140, 170, 255, 60) : QColor(255, 255, 255, 240), 1.2));
    p.drawLine(QPointF(body.left() + 14, body.top() + 1.6), QPointF(body.right() - 14, body.top() + 1.6));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ CameraFeedWidget
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_QT
struct CameraFeedWidget::CameraRig
{
    explicit CameraRig(const QCameraDevice &dev) : camera(dev) {}
    QCamera camera;
    QMediaCaptureSession session;
    QVideoSink sink;
    Decoder decoder;
};
#endif
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_OPENCV
struct CameraFeedWidget::CameraRig
{
    cv::VideoCapture capture;
    QTimer timer;
    Decoder decoder;
};
#endif

CameraFeedWidget::CameraFeedWidget(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setMinimumHeight(150);
    setMouseTracking(true);
    m_resultClock.invalidate();
    // repaint while the confirmation banner is visible so it disappears on time
    auto *t = new QTimer(this);
    connect(t, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] { if (m_resultClock.isValid()) update(); });
    t->start(300);
    // the red scan line sweeps up and down by itself while the camera runs
    auto *sweep = new QTimer(this);
    connect(sweep, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (!m_running || m_frame.isNull()) return;
        m_sweepPhase += 0.045;
        m_lineFrac = 0.5 + 0.4 * std::sin(m_sweepPhase);
        update();
    });
    sweep->start(30);
}
CameraFeedWidget::~CameraFeedWidget() = default;

// The picture is letter-boxed inside the widget; these map the aiming line to widget coordinates.
static QRect pictureRect(const QWidget *w, const QImage &img)
{
    if (img.isNull()) return w->rect();
    const QSize s = img.size().scaled(w->rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1).size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio);
    return QRect(QPoint((w->width() - s.width()) / 2, (w->height() - s.height()) / 2), s);
}

bool CameraFeedWidget::nearLine(const QPoint &p) const
{
    if (m_frame.isNull()) return false;
    const QRect pic = pictureRect(this, m_frame);
    const int y = pic.top() + static_cast<int>(pic.height() * m_lineFrac);
    return pic.contains(p) && std::abs(p.y() - y) <= 14;
}

void CameraFeedWidget::moveLine(const QPoint &p)
{
    const QRect pic = pictureRect(this, m_frame);
    m_lineFrac = qBound(0.05, double(p.y() - pic.top()) / qMax(1, pic.height()), 0.95);
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *e)
{
    Q_UNUSED(e); // the line moves by itself now
}
void CameraFeedWidget::mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *e)
{
    Q_UNUSED(e);
}
void CameraFeedWidget::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *) { m_drag = false; }

void CameraFeedWidget::showResult(const QString &text, bool ok)
{
    m_result = text;
    m_resultOk = ok;
    m_resultClock.start();
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::setRunning(bool on)
{
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
    if (!on) {
        m_rig.reset(); // stops the camera and releases the device
        m_running = false;
        m_frame = QImage();
        update();
        return;
    }
    if (m_running) return;

#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_QT
#if QT_VERSION >= QT_VERSION_CHECK(6, 5, 0)
    // Needed on macOS/mobile; on Windows it is simply granted.
    QCameraPermission permission;
    const auto status = qApp->checkPermission(permission);
    if (status == Qt::PermissionStatus::Denied) {
        emit scanError("Camera access is blocked. Allow it in Windows Settings > Privacy > Camera.");
        return;
    }
    if (status == Qt::PermissionStatus::Undetermined) {
        qApp->requestPermission(permission, this, [this](const QPermission &p) {
            if (p.status() == Qt::PermissionStatus::Granted) setRunning(true);
            else emit scanError("Camera permission was not granted.");
        });
        return;
    }
#endif
    const QCameraDevice device = QMediaDevices::defaultVideoInput();
    if (device.isNull()) {
        emit scanError("No camera found. Plug in a webcam (and close other apps that use it).");
        return;
    }
    m_rig = std::make_unique<CameraRig>(device);
    connect(&m_rig->sink, &QVideoSink::videoFrameChanged, this, [this](const QVideoFrame &f) { onImage(f.toImage()); });
    connect(&m_rig->camera, &QCamera::errorOccurred, this, [this](QCamera::Error, const QString &why) {
        setRunning(false);
        emit scanError(why.isEmpty() ? QString("The camera stopped working.") : why);
    });
    m_rig->session.setCamera(&m_rig->camera);
    m_rig->session.setVideoSink(&m_rig->sink);
    m_lastDetected.clear();
    m_lastEmit.invalidate();
    m_armed = true;
    m_decodeClock.start();
    m_running = true;
    m_rig->camera.start();
#endif
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_OPENCV
    m_rig = std::make_unique<CameraRig>();
#ifdef _WIN32
    m_rig->capture.open(0, cv::CAP_DSHOW); // DirectShow: fastest to open on Windows
    if (!m_rig->capture.isOpened()) m_rig->capture.open(0);
#else
    m_rig->capture.open(0);
#endif
    if (!m_rig->capture.isOpened()) {
        m_rig.reset();
        emit scanError("Cannot open the camera. Plug in a webcam and close other apps that use it.");
        return;
    }
    m_rig->capture.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, 640);
    m_rig->capture.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, 480);
    connect(&m_rig->timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (!m_rig) return;
        cv::Mat bgr;
        if (!m_rig->capture.read(bgr) || bgr.empty()) {
            setRunning(false);
            emit scanError("The camera stopped sending pictures.");
            return;
        }
        cv::Mat rgb;
        cv::cvtColor(bgr, rgb, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
        onImage(QImage(rgb.data, rgb.cols, rgb.rows, static_cast<int>(rgb.step), QImage::Format_RGB888).copy());
    });
    m_lastDetected.clear();
    m_lastEmit.invalidate();
    m_armed = true;
    m_decodeClock.start();
    m_running = true;
    m_rig->timer.start(33);
#endif
    update();
#else
    if (on)
        emit scanError("Camera support is not built in. Install the Qt Multimedia component and reconfigure CMake.");
#endif
}

#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
void CameraFeedWidget::onImage(const QImage &img)
{
    if (!m_running || !m_rig) return;
    if (img.isNull()) return;
    m_frame = img;
    update();
    if (m_decodeClock.elapsed() < 120) return; // decode ~8 times a second, show every frame
    m_decodeClock.restart();

    QImage g = img.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_Grayscale8);
    if (g.width() > 960) g = g.scaledToWidth(960, Qt::FastTransformation);
    GrayImage gray;
    gray.width = g.width();
    gray.height = g.height();
    gray.pixels.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(gray.width) * gray.height);
    for (int y = 0; y < gray.height; ++y)
        std::copy(g.constScanLine(y), g.constScanLine(y) + gray.width, gray.pixels.begin() + static_cast<long>(y) * gray.width);

    std::string code;
    if (m_rig->decoder.decodeCropping(gray, code, m_lineFrac)) {
        const QString value = QString::fromStdString(code);
        const auto h = m_rig->decoder.lastHit();
        if (h.valid) {
            const double k = static_cast<double>(img.width()) / gray.width; // back to camera-picture pixels
            m_hitBox = QRectF(QPointF(h.x0 * k, h.y0 * k), QPointF(h.x1 * k, h.y1 * k)).normalized();
        } else {
            m_hitBox = QRectF();
        }
        m_hitText = value;
        m_hitClock.start();
        // Scan once: report a barcode a single time, then wait until it leaves the picture.
        m_lastEmit.restart();
        if (m_armed) {
            m_armed = false;
            m_lastDetected = value;
            showResult(QString(), true); // green flash only, no text on the camera
            scanBeep();
            emit barcodeDetected(value);
        }
    } else if (!m_armed && m_lastEmit.isValid() && m_lastEmit.elapsed() > 1000) {
        m_armed = true; // no barcode for 1 second: ready for the next scan
    }
}
#endif

void CameraFeedWidget::setCode(const QString &code)
{
    m_code = code;
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    const QRect r = rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1);
    p.fillRect(r, QColor("#162644"));
    if (!m_frame.isNull()) {
        const QImage scaled = m_frame.scaled(r.size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation);
        const QPoint origin((width() - scaled.width()) / 2, (height() - scaled.height()) / 2);
        p.drawImage(origin, scaled);
        // aiming line (drag it up/down onto the barcode) + hint
        const int ly = origin.y() + static_cast<int>(scaled.height() * m_lineFrac);
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 60, 60, 220), 2));
        p.drawLine(origin.x() + 24, ly, origin.x() + scaled.width() - 24, ly);
        // soft glow trail behind the line
        for (int k = 1; k <= 10; ++k) {
            p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 60, 60, 24 - k * 2), 2));
            p.drawLine(origin.x() + 24, ly - k * 2, origin.x() + scaled.width() - 24, ly - k * 2);
        }
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setPen(Qt::white);
        // short hint on the live picture (elided so it never gets cut off)
        const QRect hint(origin.x() + 10, origin.y() + 8, scaled.width() - 20, 20);
        p.drawText(hint, Qt::AlignTop | Qt::AlignHCenter,
                   p.fontMetrics().elidedText("Hold the barcode on the red line", Qt::ElideRight, hint.width()));
        // "barcode read" confirmation
        if (m_resultClock.isValid() && m_resultClock.elapsed() < 3000) {
            const QColor c = m_resultOk ? QColor(34, 197, 94) : QColor(239, 68, 68);
            p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
            p.setPen(QPen(c, 4));
            p.drawRect(QRect(origin, scaled.size()).adjusted(2, 2, -2, -2));
        }
        // the barcode that was just read: green box for ~2.5 s (no text)
        if (m_hitClock.isValid() && m_hitClock.elapsed() < 2500 && !m_hitText.isEmpty()) {
            const double k = static_cast<double>(scaled.width()) / m_frame.width();
            if (m_hitBox.isValid() && !m_hitBox.isEmpty()) {
                const QRectF box(origin.x() + m_hitBox.x() * k, origin.y() + m_hitBox.y() * k,
                                 m_hitBox.width() * k, m_hitBox.height() * k);
                p.setBrush(QColor(40, 200, 90, 45));
                p.setPen(QPen(QColor(40, 220, 100), 3));
                p.drawRoundedRect(box.adjusted(-6, -6, 6, 6), 6, 6);
            }
        }
    } else {
        p.setPen(QColor("#c2d0ef"));
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
        p.drawText(r, Qt::AlignCenter, "Camera stopped — press Start camera");
#else
        p.drawText(r.adjusted(15, 0, -15, 0), Qt::AlignCenter | Qt::TextWordWrap,
                   "Camera unavailable in this build.\nInstall the Qt Multimedia component, then re-run CMake.\nTyped barcode lookup still works.");
#endif
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ RevenueChart (3D bars)
RevenueChart::RevenueChart(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent) {}

void RevenueChart::setData(const QStringList &labels, const QVector<double> &actual, const QVector<double> &target)
{
    m_labels = labels;
    m_actual = actual;
    m_target = target;
    update();
}

void RevenueChart::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    const int n = m_actual.size();
    if (n < 2)
        return;
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);

    const qreal depth = 7;
    const QRectF area(66, 12 + depth, width() - 66 - 10 - depth, height() - 12 - depth - 24);
    double maxV = 1;
    for (double v : m_actual)
        maxV = qMax(maxV, v);
    for (double v : m_target)
        maxV = qMax(maxV, v);
    maxV = qCeil(maxV * 1.1 / 200.0) * 200.0;

    QFont f = font();
    f.setPixelSize(11);
    p.setFont(f);
    // grid + y labels
    for (int g = 0; g <= 4; ++g)
    {
        const qreal y = area.bottom() - area.height() * g / 4.0;
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor("#e1e8f8"), 1, g == 0 ? Qt::SolidLine : Qt::DashLine));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(area.left(), y), QPointF(area.right() + depth, y));
        p.setPen(Theme::outline);
        p.drawText(QRectF(0, y - 8, 60, 16), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, QString("PKR %1").arg(int(maxV * g / 4)));
    }

    const qreal slot = area.width() / n;
    const qreal bw = slot * 0.54;
    int peak = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i)
        if (m_actual[i] > m_actual[peak])
            peak = i;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
    {
        const bool hot = i == peak;
        const qreal x = area.left() + slot * i + (slot - bw) / 2;
        const qreal h = qMax<qreal>(3, area.height() * m_actual[i] / maxV);
        const qreal y = area.bottom() - h;
        const QColor c1 = hot ? QColor("#7fb0ff") : QColor("#7aa6f7");
        const QColor c2 = hot ? QColor("#2457e6") : QColor("#3f6fe0");
        // contact shadow
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(QColor(20, 40, 110, 40));
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(x - 2, area.bottom() - 3, bw + depth + 4, 7));
        // front
        QLinearGradient fg(x, y, x, y + h);
        fg.setColorAt(0, c1);
        fg.setColorAt(1, c2);
        p.setBrush(fg);
        p.drawRect(QRectF(x, y, bw, h));
        // side
        p.setBrush(c2.darker(135));
        p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{x + bw, y}, {x + bw + depth, y - depth}, {x + bw + depth, y + h - depth}, {x + bw, y + h}}));
        // top
        p.setBrush(c1.lighter(125));
        p.drawPolygon(QPolygonF({{x, y}, {x + depth, y - depth}, {x + bw + depth, y - depth}, {x + bw, y}}));
        // gloss strip
        p.setBrush(QColor(255, 255, 255, 45));
        p.drawRect(QRectF(x + 2, y + 2, bw * 0.25, h - 4));

        if (hot)
        {
            p.setPen(Theme::primary);
            QFont bf = f;
            bf.setBold(true);
            p.setFont(bf);
            p.drawText(QRectF(x - 30, y - depth - 18, bw + depth + 60, 16), Qt::AlignCenter, QString("PKR %1").arg(int(m_actual[i])));
            p.setFont(f);
        }
        p.setPen(hot ? Theme::primary : Theme::onSurfaceVariant);
        if (i < m_labels.size())
        {
            QFont lf = f;
            lf.setBold(hot);
            p.setFont(lf);
            p.drawText(QRectF(x - 6, area.bottom() + 6, bw + depth + 12, 16), Qt::AlignCenter, m_labels[i]);
            p.setFont(f);
        }
    }

    // target line
    if (m_target.size() == n)
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
        {
            const qreal x = area.left() + slot * i + slot / 2 + depth / 2;
            const qreal y = area.bottom() - area.height() * m_target[i] / maxV - depth / 2;
            if (i == 0)
                path.moveTo(x, y);
            else
                path.lineTo(x, y);
        }
        p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor("#f08a24"), 2, Qt::DashLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin));
        p.drawPath(path);
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ QtyStepper
QtyStepper::QtyStepper(int value, QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(this);
    h->setContentsMargins(4, 2, 4, 2);
    h->setSpacing(6);
    auto *m = W::button("−", "step");
    auto *pl = W::button("+", "step");
    auto *v = W::label(QString::number(value));
    v->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    v->setMinimumWidth(22);
    v->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700; color:#004ac6;");
    h->addWidget(m);
    h->addWidget(v);
    h->addWidget(pl);
    connect(m, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QtyStepper::minus);
    connect(pl, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QtyStepper::plus);
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/main.cpp

#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QFont>
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include <QStyleFactory>

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    QApplication::setApplicationName("BarcodeHub");
    QApplication::setStyle(QStyleFactory::create("Fusion")); // identical look on every platform
    QFont f = app.font();
    f.setPointSizeF(9.5);
    app.setFont(f);
    app.setStyleSheet(Theme::styleSheet());

    // Sign in, run the POS, and return to sign-in after "Log out".
    while (true)
    {
        UserRole role = UserRole::Cashier;
        QString name;
        if (!Dialogs::login(nullptr, role, name))
            return 0; // sign-in window closed
        MainWindow w(role, name);
        w.show();
        app.exec();
        if (!w.loggedOut())
            return 0; // "Exit" closes the app
    }
}

# Increment 1 — Barcode Generator and Reader

---

## Overview

The first increment focuses on developing a working version of the C++ desktop application with barcode generation and reading capabilities.

The application must be able to generate a barcode from a six-digit number, display it on the screen, save it as an image, and read the generated barcode through either an uploaded image or a laptop camera.

---

## Requirements

### 1. Barcode Generation

The application must:

- Accept a six-digit numeric input from the user.
- Ensure that the first digit is non-zero.
- Generate a barcode corresponding to the provided number.
- Display the generated barcode on the application screen.
- Save the generated barcode to disk as an image.

### 2. Barcode Reading

The application must be able to read a barcode generated through the application.

**A. Image Upload**

- Allow the user to select and upload a barcode image.
- Read and decode the barcode from the selected image.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

**B. Laptop Camera**

- Allow the user to scan a barcode using the laptop camera.
- Detect and decode the barcode from the camera feed.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

---

## Technical Constraints

- Existing non-standard libraries must not be used for barcode generation.
- Libraries for image processing, file handling, and other supporting operations are permitted.
- The application must be developed in C++.
- All submissions must be made through GitHub.

---

## Team Contributions

### Farzam — UI Development & Barcode Reading

- UI Development (with Ali)
- Barcode Decoding
- Decoded Value Return Handling
- Invalid Input Handling

### Ali — UI Development & Barcode Generation

- UI Development (with Farzam)
- Barcode Reading
- Image Processing
- Input Handling
- Taking 6-Digit Number as Input

### Mustafa — Project Lead, Management & Integration

- Project Management
- Project Integration
- Error Handling
- CMake Build Configuration

### Shauzab — Camera & Live Scanner Development

- Barcode Reading (with Farzam)
- Live Scanner Implementation
- Camera Opening and Initialization
- Camera Frame Capturing
- Scanner Functionality

---

## Notes

- The generated barcode must represent the original six-digit input.
- Both image upload and laptop camera scanning are required reading methods.
- Further details about the products, files, and their formats will be provided after Increment 1.

# Increment 2 — Point of Sale (POS) System

## Overview

Increment 2 extends BarcodeHub from a barcode generation and reading application into a GUI-based Point of Sale (POS) system.

The system will allow cashiers to scan product barcodes, retrieve product information, manage orders, calculate bills, and record payment details.

## Objectives

- Implement a camera-based barcode reading workflow.
- Develop a GUI-based POS interface.
- Retrieve product specifications from `Product.txt`.
- Manage shopping carts and order records.
- Validate product quantities against available stock.
- Generate and save bills.
- Support different payment modes.
- Maintain modular, reusable, and maintainable code.

## Team Division & Responsibilities

### 1. Farzam Zeeshan — Product Management

**Responsibilities**
- Read and parse `Product.txt`.
- Create the `Product` class.
- Search products by barcode and product ID.
- Display product descriptions.
- Validate available product stock.

**Deliverable:** Product management module with `Product.txt` integration.

### 2. Shauzab Mukhtar — Camera & Scanner

**Responsibilities**
- Implement camera-based barcode scanning.
- Integrate the scanner with the POS workflow.
- Handle camera and barcode scanning errors.
- Reuse the barcode reading functionality developed in Increment 1.

**Deliverable:** Camera scanner module.

### 3. Muhammad Mustafa Amir — Order Management

**Responsibilities**
- Record orders and manage the shopping cart.
- Validate requested quantities against available stock.
- Generate unique 8-digit Order IDs.
- Save order records in `Order.txt`.
- Save product details and quantities in `OrderDetail.txt`.

**Deliverable:** Order management module.

### 4. Ali Abdullah — Billing & POS GUI

**Responsibilities**
- Design and implement the POS graphical interface.
- Generate bills and calculate bill totals.
- Add the Description button to the order-recording interface.
- Save generated bills in `Bill.txt`.
- Implement payment modes.

**Deliverable:** POS GUI and billing module.

## Integration Flow

The planned module integration sequence is:

1. **Product Management:** Retrieve product information and validate stock.
2. **Camera & Scanner:** Scan product barcodes and provide decoded barcode values to the POS workflow.
3. **Order Management:** Add products to the cart and manage order records.
4. **Billing:** Calculate totals, generate bills, and record payment details.

## Data File Specifications

### Product.txt

Each product occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and must follow this order:

1. Product ID
2. Product barcode
3. Product acronym (maximum 8 characters)
4. Product name
5. Measurement unit (`count` or `weight`)
6. Product unit price
7. Product tax amount in PKR
8. Discount per unit
9. Available quantity
10. Barcode file name and path

### Order.txt

Each order occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and follow this order:

1. 8-digit Order ID (last two digits indicate the order date)
2. Order date
3. Order time
4. Customer name
5. Customer contact (optional)
6. Order status (`in-progress`, `on-hold`, `paid`, `archived`)
7. Payment amount
8. Payment mode (`cash`, `card`, `wallet`)
9. Payment merchant name

### OrderDetail.txt

Each order detail record occupies one line and ends with `#`.

Fields:

1. Order ID
2. Product ID
3. Product barcode
4. Quantity

Each record associates a product and its quantity with an order.

### Bill.txt

Each bill occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and follow this order:

1. 9-digit Bill ID
2. Customer name
3. Order ID
4. Product acronym
5. Product unit price without tax
6. Discount per unit (if applicable)
7. Product quantity
8. Total bill without tax
9. Total tax
10. Total discount
11. Total payable bill
12. Bill payment status (order status from `Order.txt`)

## Software Engineering Principles

The implementation should emphasize:

- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** Each class and module should have one clear responsibility.
- **Open/Closed Principle (OCP):** Components should support extension without unnecessary modification to existing functionality.
- **Code Quality:** Keep code readable, consistent, and maintainable.
- **Reusability:** Reuse existing barcode functionality and design independent modules with clear interfaces.
