FILE: /Project Planning/Increment-3.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Increment 3 — Inventory, Discounts, Reporting & Advanced Billing

## Overview

Increment 3 adds product categories, inventory management, seasonal and special discounts, detailed billing, PDF bill printing, and management reports to the Point of Sale (PoS) system.

Managers can configure product categories, generate category-based barcodes, add and edit products, manage inventory, set discount seasons, and generate reports. Cashiers can record, hold, resume, and edit orders with a minimum number of clicks. Both cashiers and managers can print bills as PDF files.

## Objectives

- Introduce product categories and category-based barcode generation.
- Allow managers to add and edit products through the GUI.
- Allow managers to view and update inventory.
- Keep the available quantity of every product up to date with each order.
- Allow existing orders to be opened and edited under controlled rules.
- Store detailed bill information in `Bill.txt` and `BillDetail.txt`.
- Support seasonal discounts and special discounts.
- Print bills as PDF.
- Provide tabular management reports.
- Provide separate cashier and manager features in the user interface.
- Keep order recording fast, with a minimum number of clicks and button presses.

## Functional Requirements

### A. Product Categories & Barcode Generation

1. The manager shall be able to configure product categories through the GUI before generating barcodes.
2. A file `PCategory.txt` shall be maintained. Each line of the file, ending with `#`, is one product category.
3. The first two digits of a product barcode shall represent the product category (the Category ID).
4. A manager shall be able to generate a barcode for a product and add new products to `Product.txt`.
5. The Category ID shall be stored in `Product.txt` against each product.
6. A manager shall be able to add or edit any product.

### B. Orders & Inventory

7. An existing order can be opened and edited by a cashier.
8. An order with `paid` status can be edited within 7 days of the order payment, and only after approval by a manager. This is different from the handling of orders that are `on-hold`.
9. A manager can see the inventory of the products at any time, as a list of products with their available quantities.
10. A manager can update the inventory.
11. The available quantity of products shall be updated with every order.

### C. Billing, Discounts & Printing

12. A bill can be printed as a PDF by a cashier or a manager.
13. `Bill.txt` stores one bill per line (see Data File Specifications).
14. `BillDetail.txt` stores the bill lines showing the product information included in each bill.
15. The manager shall set seasons for special discounts by saving the start date, end date, and discount percentage of each season in `Seasons.txt`.
16. A manager can add new discounts through the user interface and set a discount active or inactive in `Seasons.txt`.
17. When a bill is generated, the system shall calculate the final amount based on the discount percentage if the current date (the date on which the bill is made) is within the dates mentioned in `Seasons.txt`. The bill shall mention this amount in the seasonal discount field.
18. A special discount, not more than 8 percent, can be applied to a bill.

### D. Reports

19. The manager shall be able to generate the reports listed in the Reports section, in tabular form.

### E. Usability & Roles

20. Recording an order shall need a minimum number of clicks and button presses. Extra clicks or button presses while scanning a product barcode are not desired.
21. Putting an order on hold shall be easy and shall need a minimum number of clicks and button presses.
22. An on-hold order shall be resumable with minimum user interaction.
23. The application shall provide features to the cashier and to the manager separately through the user interface.

## Roles & Access

| Feature | Cashier | Manager |
|---|:---:|:---:|
| Open and edit an existing order | Yes | |
| Approve the editing of a `paid` order | | Yes |
| Print a bill as PDF | Yes | Yes |
| Configure product categories | | Yes |
| Generate barcodes and add products | | Yes |
| Add or edit any product | | Yes |
| View and update inventory | | Yes |
| Set seasons and add or activate discounts | | Yes |
| Generate reports | | Yes |

## Reports

All reports are generated by the manager and shown in tabular form.

| # | Report | Content |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Incomplete orders | All orders that are not `paid` or `archived`, with the total amount of all such bills |
| 2 | Completed orders | All orders that have been completed, with the total amount of these bills |
| 3 | Cash sales | All cash sales for a selected period: daily, weekly, monthly, yearly, or a specified time period |
| 4 | All sales | All sales with the total amount and the discounts |
| 5 | Top ten most sold items | The ten most sold items in descending order, with their remaining stock |
| 6 | Top ten items by remaining stock | The ten items with the highest remaining quantity, in descending order, so that their purchase is not initiated |

## Data File Specifications

Each record occupies one line. Fields are space-separated.

### PCategory.txt

Each category occupies one line ending with `#`. Fields:

1. Category ID (2 digits; used when generating barcodes)
2. Category name
3. Category acronym

Example line:

```text
10 Beverages BEV #
```

### Product.txt

The Category ID of the product is stored in `Product.txt` against each product.

### Bill.txt

Each bill occupies one line. Fields, in this order:

1. Bill ID (9 digits, unique, cannot repeat in the file)
2. Customer name
3. Order ID (from `Order.txt`)
4. Total bill without tax
5. Total tax
6. Total applicable discount (the sum of the discounts on all bought products)
7. Total payable bill
8. Amount received
9. Balance returned
10. Bill payment status (the order status from `Order.txt`)
11. Seasonal discount
12. Special discount (not more than 8 percent)
13. Bill date
14. Bill time

### BillDetail.txt

Each record is one bill line showing a product included in a bill. Fields, in this order:

1. Bill ID (9 digits, from `Bill.txt`)
2. Product acronym
3. Product unit price without tax
4. Product quantity
5. Discount on the bought product (for example, if 10 products are bought with a discount of 5 rupees on each, this field stores 50)

### Seasons.txt

Each season occupies one line ending with `#`. Fields, in this order:

1. Start date
2. End date
3. Discount percentage
4. isActive (`true` or `false`)

## Business Rules

- The first two digits of every barcode are the Category ID of the product.
- A `paid` order can be edited only within 7 days of its payment and only after manager approval.
- The available quantity of a product is updated with every order.
- A Bill ID is a unique 9-digit number and never repeats in `Bill.txt`.
- The seasonal discount is applied when the bill date lies within the start and end dates of an active season in `Seasons.txt`.
- The special discount cannot exceed 8 percent.
- The discount stored in `BillDetail.txt` is the total discount for the product line (unit discount multiplied by quantity).

## Software Engineering Principles

The implementation should emphasize:

- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** Each class and module should have one clear responsibility, for example category handling, inventory, discount calculation, and report generation as separate components.
- **Open/Closed Principle (OCP):** Components should support extension, such as new reports or new discount types, without unnecessary modification of existing functionality.
- **Code Quality:** Keep code readable, consistent, and maintainable.
- **Reusability:** Design independent modules with clear interfaces.
- **Testing:** Every module includes basic tests, and `CMakeLists.txt` is updated whenever new source files are added.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /scripts/build-windows.ps1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
param(
    [string]$QtRoot,
    [string]$MinGWRoot,
    [int]$Jobs = 4,
    [switch]$SkipTests
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$repo = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$deps = Join-Path $repo 'build/deps'
$build = Join-Path $repo 'build/local-release'
$package = Join-Path $repo 'build/portable/BarcodeHub'
function Run([string]$Program, [string[]]$Arguments) {
    & $Program @Arguments
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Program failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE" }
}

if (!$QtRoot) {
    $kits = @(Get-ChildItem 'C:/Qt/6.*/mingw_64','D:/Qt/6.*/mingw_64' -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Sort-Object { [version]$_.Parent.Name } -Descending)
    if (!$kits.Count) { throw 'Install Qt 6 for MinGW with its matching compiler, or pass -QtRoot and -MinGWRoot.' }
    $QtRoot = $kits[0].FullName
}
$qtBase = Split-Path (Split-Path $QtRoot -Parent) -Parent
$config = Get-Content (Join-Path $QtRoot 'mkspecs/qconfig.pri') -Raw
$gccMajor = [regex]::Match($config, 'QT_GCC_MAJOR_VERSION = (\d+)').Groups[1].Value
$gccMinor = [regex]::Match($config, 'QT_GCC_MINOR_VERSION = (\d+)').Groups[1].Value
if (!$MinGWRoot) {
    foreach ($candidate in Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $qtBase 'Tools/mingw*') -Directory) {
        $compiler = Join-Path $candidate.FullName 'bin/g++.exe'
        if (!(Test-Path $compiler)) { continue }
        $version = & $compiler -dumpfullversion
        if ($version -like "$gccMajor.$gccMinor.*") { $MinGWRoot = $candidate.FullName; break }
    }
}
if (!$MinGWRoot) { throw 'No matching Qt MinGW compiler found. Pass -MinGWRoot explicitly.' }
$cmake = Join-Path $qtBase 'Tools/CMake_64/bin/cmake.exe'
$ninja = Join-Path $qtBase 'Tools/Ninja/ninja.exe'
$deploy = Join-Path $QtRoot 'bin/windeployqt.exe'
foreach ($file in @($cmake,$ninja,$deploy,(Join-Path $MinGWRoot 'bin/g++.exe'))) {
    if (!(Test-Path $file)) { throw "Missing dependency: $file" }
}
$originalPath = $env:PATH
try {
    $env:PATH = "$MinGWRoot/bin;$QtRoot/bin;$(Split-Path $ninja -Parent);$originalPath"
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $deps,$package | Out-Null
    $opencv = Join-Path $deps 'opencv'
    if (!(Test-Path (Join-Path $opencv 'OpenCVConfig.cmake'))) {
        $source = Join-Path $deps 'opencv-src'
        if (!(Test-Path (Join-Path $source 'CMakeLists.txt'))) {
            $archive = Join-Path $deps 'opencv-4.13.0.zip'
            $source = Join-Path $deps 'opencv-4.13.0'
            if (!(Test-Path (Join-Path $source 'CMakeLists.txt'))) {
                Write-Host 'Downloading official OpenCV 4.13.0 sources...'
                Invoke-WebRequest 'https://codeload.github.com/opencv/opencv/zip/refs/tags/4.13.0' -OutFile $archive
                Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $archive -DestinationPath $deps -Force
            }
        }
        # Some existing source caches contain only the modules used by this app.
        $dlpackLicense = Join-Path $source '3rdparty/dlpack/LICENSE'
        if (!(Test-Path $dlpackLicense)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $dlpackLicense -Parent) | Out-Null
            Invoke-WebRequest 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/opencv/opencv/4.13.0/3rdparty/dlpack/LICENSE' -OutFile $dlpackLicense
        }
        $opencvBuild = Join-Path $deps 'opencv-build'
        # OpenCV 4.13 uses non-standard M_PI here, unavailable in strict MinGW C++.
        # Its own CV_PI constant is equivalent and portable.
        $ellipse = Join-Path $source 'modules/imgproc/src/shapedescr.cpp'
        $ellipseText = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($ellipse)
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($ellipse, $ellipseText.Replace('angle_deg * M_PI', 'angle_deg * CV_PI'))
        Run $cmake @('-S',$source,'-B',$opencvBuild,'-G','Ninja',
            '-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release',"-DCMAKE_C_COMPILER=$MinGWRoot/bin/gcc.exe",
            "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=$MinGWRoot/bin/g++.exe","-DCMAKE_MAKE_PROGRAM=$ninja",
            "-DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX=$opencv",'-DBUILD_LIST=core,imgproc,imgcodecs,videoio',
            '-DBUILD_SHARED_LIBS=ON','-DBUILD_TESTS=OFF','-DBUILD_PERF_TESTS=OFF',
            '-DBUILD_EXAMPLES=OFF','-DBUILD_opencv_apps=OFF','-DBUILD_JAVA=OFF',
            '-DBUILD_opencv_python3=OFF','-DWITH_FFMPEG=OFF','-DWITH_MSMF=OFF',
            '-DWITH_DSHOW=ON','-DWITH_IPP=OFF','-DWITH_OPENCL=OFF','-DWITH_OPENEXR=OFF',
            '-DWITH_WEBP=OFF','-DWITH_AVIF=OFF','-DWITH_JASPER=OFF','-DWITH_TIFF=OFF',
            '-DWITH_OPENJPEG=OFF','-DWITH_PROTOBUF=OFF','-DWITH_FLATBUFFERS=OFF','-DCPU_DISPATCH=')
        Run $cmake @('--build',$opencvBuild,'--parallel',"$Jobs")
        Run $cmake @('--install',$opencvBuild)
    }
    Run $cmake @('-S',$repo,'-B',$build,'-G','Ninja','-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release',
        "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=$MinGWRoot/bin/g++.exe","-DCMAKE_MAKE_PROGRAM=$ninja",
        "-DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH=$QtRoot","-DOpenCV_DIR=$opencv",'-DCAMERA=ON','-DBUILD_TESTING=ON')
    Run $cmake @('--build',$build,'--parallel',"$Jobs")
    Run $deploy @('--release','--no-translations','--no-system-d3d-compiler','--no-opengl-sw',
        '--compiler-runtime',"$build/BarcodeHub.exe")
    Copy-Item (Join-Path $QtRoot 'plugins/platforms/qoffscreen.dll') (Join-Path $build 'platforms/qoffscreen.dll') -Force
    if (!$SkipTests) {
        Run (Join-Path (Split-Path $cmake -Parent) 'ctest.exe') @('--test-dir',$build,'--output-on-failure')
    }
    $builtExe = Join-Path $build 'BarcodeHub.exe'
    $portableExe = Join-Path $package 'BarcodeHub.exe'
    try {
        Run $cmake @('-E','copy_if_different',$builtExe,$portableExe)
    } catch {
        # Windows permits renaming an executing image. Keep that session intact
        # and retain its executable while publishing the new build for next launch.
        if (!(Test-Path $portableExe)) { throw }
        $previousExe = Join-Path $package ("BarcodeHub.previous-" + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '.exe')
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $portableExe -Destination $previousExe
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $builtExe -Destination $portableExe
        Write-Host "Running session preserved at $previousExe; restart BarcodeHub to use the new build."
    }
    foreach ($dll in Get-ChildItem $build -Filter '*.dll') {
        Run $cmake @('-E','copy_if_different',$dll.FullName,(Join-Path $package $dll.Name))
    }
    # OpenCV DLLs are installed under the OpenCV prefix, not deployed by windeployqt.
    foreach ($cvBin in @((Join-Path $opencv 'bin'), (Join-Path $opencv 'x64/mingw/bin'))) {
        if (Test-Path $cvBin) {
            Get-ChildItem $cvBin -Filter '*.dll' -File | ForEach-Object {
                Run $cmake @('-E','copy_if_different',$_.FullName,(Join-Path $package $_.Name))
            }
        }
    }
    Run $deploy @('--release','--no-translations','--no-system-d3d-compiler','--no-opengl-sw',
        '--compiler-runtime',"$package/BarcodeHub.exe")
    # Seed only missing files; preserve orders, stock, and bills in an existing installation.
    Run $cmake @("-DSOURCE_DATA=$repo/data", "-DDEST_DATA=$package/data",
        '-P', (Join-Path $repo 'cmake/seed-data.cmake'))
    $licenses = Join-Path $package 'licenses'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $licenses | Out-Null
    Copy-Item (Join-Path $repo 'LICENSE') (Join-Path $licenses 'BarcodeHub-LICENSE.txt') -Force
    Copy-Item (Join-Path $opencv 'LICENSE') (Join-Path $licenses 'OpenCV-LICENSE.txt') -Force
    Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $opencv 'etc/licenses') -File -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Copy-Item -Destination $licenses -Force
    $qtLicenses = Join-Path $qtBase 'Licenses'
    if (Test-Path $qtLicenses) {
        $qtLicenseTarget = Join-Path $licenses 'Qt'
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $qtLicenseTarget | Out-Null
        Get-ChildItem $qtLicenses -File | Copy-Item -Destination $qtLicenseTarget -Force
    }
    Write-Host "Ready: $package/BarcodeHub.exe"
} finally {
    $env:PATH = $originalPath
}

FILE: /tests/billing_tests.cpp

/**
 * @file billing_tests.cpp
 * @brief Comprehensive unit test suite for the BarcodeHub Billing subsystem.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Verifies calculation accuracy, monetary rounding, error validation,
 * payment strategy processing, record codec serialization, and repository persistence.
 */

#include "billing/Bill.h"
#include "billing/BillCalculator.h"
#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"
#include "billing/PaymentMethod.h"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace fs = std::filesystem;
using namespace barcodehub::billing;

#define TEST_ASSERT(cond, msg) \
    do { \
        if (!(cond)) { \
            std::cerr << "[-] ASSERTION FAILED: " << msg \
                      << " (" << __FILE__ << ":" << __LINE__ << ")\n"; \
            return false; \
        } \
    } while (0)

#define RUN_TEST(fn) \
    do { \
        std::cout << "[RUN] " #fn "... "; \
        if (fn()) { \
            std::cout << "PASSED\n"; \
            ++passedTests; \
        } else { \
            std::cout << "FAILED\n"; \
            ++failedTests; \
        } \
        ++totalTests; \
    } while (0)

namespace {

bool doubleNear(double a, double b, double epsilon = 0.001) {
    return std::abs(a - b) <= epsilon;
}

// ============================================================
// BILL CALCULATOR TESTS
// ============================================================

bool testCalculatorSingleItem() {
    std::vector<BillLine> lines = {
        {"MILK", 100.0, 17.0, 5.0, 2.0} // 2 units: subtotal 200, tax 34, discount 10 -> payable 224
    };

    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(BillCalculator::calculate(lines, totals, &error), "Single item calculation failed: " + error);
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.withoutTax, 200.0), "Subtotal mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.tax, 34.0), "Tax mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.discount, 10.0), "Discount mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.payable, 224.0), "Payable mismatch");
    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorMultipleItems() {
    std::vector<BillLine> lines = {
        {"BREAD", 150.0, 0.0, 10.0, 1.0},   // subtotal 150, tax 0, disc 10
        {"EGG", 25.0, 2.5, 0.0, 12.0},      // subtotal 300, tax 30, disc 0
        {"APPLE", 200.0, 10.0, 5.0, 1.5}    // subtotal 300, tax 15, disc 7.5
    };

    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(BillCalculator::calculate(lines, totals, &error), "Multi-item calculation failed: " + error);
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.withoutTax, 750.0), "Multi-item subtotal mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.tax, 45.0), "Multi-item tax mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.discount, 17.5), "Multi-item discount mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.payable, 777.5), "Multi-item payable mismatch");
    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorRoundingPrecision() {
    std::vector<BillLine> lines = {
        {"RICE", 33.3333, 1.6666, 0.5555, 3.0}
    };

    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(BillCalculator::calculate(lines, totals, &error), "Rounding test failed: " + error);
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.withoutTax, 100.0, 0.01), "Rounded withoutTax mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.tax, 5.0, 0.01), "Rounded tax mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.discount, 1.67, 0.01), "Rounded discount mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(totals.payable, 103.33, 0.01), "Rounded payable mismatch");
    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorEmptyLines() {
    std::vector<BillLine> lines;
    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(lines, totals, &error), "Empty bill should fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("at least one product") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);
    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorNegativeValues() {
    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;

    std::vector<BillLine> negPrice = {{"ITEM", -50.0, 5.0, 0.0, 1.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(negPrice, totals, &error), "Negative price should fail");

    std::vector<BillLine> negTax = {{"ITEM", 50.0, -5.0, 0.0, 1.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(negTax, totals, &error), "Negative tax should fail");

    std::vector<BillLine> negDisc = {{"ITEM", 50.0, 5.0, -2.0, 1.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(negDisc, totals, &error), "Negative discount should fail");

    std::vector<BillLine> zeroQty = {{"ITEM", 50.0, 5.0, 0.0, 0.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(zeroQty, totals, &error), "Zero quantity should fail");

    std::vector<BillLine> negQty = {{"ITEM", 50.0, 5.0, 0.0, -3.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(negQty, totals, &error), "Negative quantity should fail");

    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorExcessiveDiscount() {
    std::vector<BillLine> lines = {
        {"COFFEE", 100.0, 0.0, 150.0, 1.0}
    };

    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(lines, totals, &error), "Excessive discount should be rejected");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("Discount is larger") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);
    return true;
}

bool testCalculatorNonFiniteValues() {
    BillTotals totals;
    std::string error;
    double nanVal = std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN();
    double infVal = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();

    std::vector<BillLine> nanLine = {{"NAN_ITEM", nanVal, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(nanLine, totals, &error), "NaN price should be rejected");

    std::vector<BillLine> infLine = {{"INF_ITEM", 10.0, infVal, 0.0, 1.0}};
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillCalculator::calculate(infLine, totals, &error), "Infinity tax should be rejected");

    return true;
}

// ============================================================
// PAYMENT STRATEGY TESTS
// ============================================================

bool testCashPaymentExact() {
    CashPayment cash;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    req.amountTendered = 500.0;

    PaymentResult res = cash.process(req, 500.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(res.ok, "Exact cash should succeed");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(res.change, 0.0), "Change should be 0 for exact payment");
    return true;
}

bool testCashPaymentWithChange() {
    CashPayment cash;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    req.amountTendered = 1000.0;

    PaymentResult res = cash.process(req, 777.50);
    TEST_ASSERT(res.ok, "Overpayment should succeed");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(res.change, 222.50), "Change should be 222.50");
    return true;
}

bool testCashPaymentInsufficient() {
    CashPayment cash;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    req.amountTendered = 450.0;

    PaymentResult res = cash.process(req, 500.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(!res.ok, "Insufficient cash should be rejected");
    TEST_ASSERT(res.error.find("less than the amount payable") != std::string::npos,
                "Unexpected error message: " + res.error);

    // Negative cash tendered
    req.amountTendered = -50.0;
    PaymentResult resNeg = cash.process(req, 500.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(!resNeg.ok, "Negative cash tendered must be rejected");
    TEST_ASSERT(resNeg.error.find("Invalid cash amount") != std::string::npos,
                "Unexpected error message: " + resNeg.error);
    return true;
}

bool testCardPaymentValid() {
    CardPayment card;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Card;
    req.merchant = "HBL";

    PaymentResult res = card.process(req, 1250.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(res.ok, "Card payment with valid bank should succeed");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(res.change, 0.0), "Card payment change should be zero");
    return true;
}

bool testCardPaymentMissingBank() {
    CardPayment card;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Card;
    req.merchant = "";

    PaymentResult res = card.process(req, 1250.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(!res.ok, "Card payment without bank name should fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(res.error.find("bank") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + res.error);

    // Whitespace-only name should also fail
    req.merchant = "   \t  ";
    PaymentResult resWs = card.process(req, 1250.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(!resWs.ok, "Card payment with whitespace bank name should fail");
    return true;
}

bool testCardPaymentInvalidCharacter() {
    CardPayment card;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Card;
    req.merchant = "Bank#Branch"; // '#' is forbidden delimiter

    PaymentResult res = card.process(req, 1250.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(!res.ok, "Merchant with '#' delimiter should be rejected");
    return true;
}

bool testWalletPaymentValid() {
    WalletPayment wallet;
    PaymentRequest req;
    req.mode = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Wallet;
    req.merchant = "JazzCash";

    PaymentResult res = wallet.process(req, 350.0);
    TEST_ASSERT(res.ok, "Wallet payment with valid provider should succeed");
    return true;
}

bool testPaymentRegistryDefaults() {
    PaymentMethodRegistry reg = PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults();
    TEST_ASSERT(reg.find(barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Cash) != nullptr, "Cash method must be registered");
    TEST_ASSERT(reg.find(barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Card) != nullptr, "Card method must be registered");
    TEST_ASSERT(reg.find(barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Wallet) != nullptr, "Wallet method must be registered");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================
// BILL RECORD CODEC TESTS
// ============================================================

bool testCodecFormatAndParse() {
    Bill bill;
    bill.id = "261118001";
    bill.customerName = "Ali Abdullah";
    bill.orderId = "26111818";
    bill.paymentStatus = "paid";
    bill.totals = {500.0, 50.0, 25.0, 525.0};

    BillLine line;
    line.productAcronym = "MILK";
    line.unitPrice = 250.0;
    line.discountPerUnit = 12.5;
    line.quantity = 2.0;

    std::string formatted = BillRecordCodec::format(bill, line);
    TEST_ASSERT(formatted.back() == '#', "Formatted record must end with '#'");

    Bill parsedHeader;
    BillLine parsedLine;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(BillRecordCodec::parse(formatted, parsedHeader, parsedLine, &error),
                "Parse failed: " + error);

    TEST_ASSERT(parsedHeader.id == "261118001", "Parsed bill ID mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(parsedHeader.customerName == "Ali Abdullah", "Parsed customer mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(parsedHeader.orderId == "26111818", "Parsed order ID mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(parsedHeader.paymentStatus == "paid", "Parsed status mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(parsedHeader.totals.payable, 525.0), "Parsed payable mismatch");

    TEST_ASSERT(parsedLine.productAcronym == "MILK", "Parsed acronym mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(parsedLine.unitPrice, 250.0), "Parsed unit price mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(parsedLine.discountPerUnit, 12.5), "Parsed discount mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(parsedLine.quantity, 2.0), "Parsed quantity mismatch");
    return true;
}

bool testCodecParseErrors() {
    Bill header;
    BillLine line;
    std::string error;

    std::string noTerminator = "261118001 Ali 26111818 MILK 250 12.5 2 500 50 25 525 paid";
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillRecordCodec::parse(noTerminator, header, line, &error), "Missing '#' must fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("end with '#'") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);

    std::string shortRecord = "261118001 Ali 26111818 MILK #";
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillRecordCodec::parse(shortRecord, header, line, &error), "Short record must fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("expected 12 fields") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);

    std::string badId = "26111801 Ali 26111818 MILK 250 12.5 2 500 50 25 525 paid #";
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillRecordCodec::parse(badId, header, line, &error), "8-digit bill ID must fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("exactly 9 digits") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);

    std::string badNumber = "261118001 Ali 26111818 MILK NOT_A_NUM 12.5 2 500 50 25 525 paid #";
    TEST_ASSERT(!BillRecordCodec::parse(badNumber, header, line, &error), "Non-numeric price must fail");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("not a number") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);

    return true;
}

// ============================================================
// BILL REPOSITORY TESTS
// ============================================================

bool testRepositoryNextBillId() {
    std::vector<Bill> existing;
    std::string error;

    // 1. First bill of the day
    std::string id1 = BillRepository::nextBillId("2026-11-18", existing, &error);
    TEST_ASSERT(id1 == "261118001", "Expected 261118001, got: " + id1);

    // 2. Sequential increment
    Bill b1;
    b1.id = id1;
    existing.push_back(b1);
    std::string id2 = BillRepository::nextBillId("2026-11-18", existing, &error);
    TEST_ASSERT(id2 == "261118002", "Expected 261118002, got: " + id2);

    // 3. Different date does not interfere
    Bill bPrevDate;
    bPrevDate.id = "261117099"; // previous day had 99 bills
    existing.push_back(bPrevDate);
    std::string idNewDate = BillRepository::nextBillId("2026-11-18", existing, &error);
    TEST_ASSERT(idNewDate == "261118002", "Previous date should not increment current date sequence");

    // 4. Invalid date format
    std::string badDateId = BillRepository::nextBillId("18-11-2026", existing, &error);
    TEST_ASSERT(badDateId.empty(), "Invalid date format should fail");

    return true;
}

bool testRepositoryAppendAndLoad() {
    const fs::path testDir = fs::temp_directory_path() / "barcodehub_billing_unit_test";
    std::error_code ec;
    fs::remove_all(testDir, ec);
    fs::create_directories(testDir, ec);

    const fs::path billFile = testDir / "Bill.txt";
    BillRepository repo(billFile);

    std::vector<Bill> initial;
    std::string error;
    TEST_ASSERT(repo.loadAll(initial, &error), "Load from non-existent file should return empty vector");
    TEST_ASSERT(initial.empty(), "Initial bills must be empty");

    // Create a 2-line bill
    Bill bill;
    bill.id = "261118001";
    bill.customerName = "Ali Abdullah";
    bill.orderId = "26111818";
    bill.paymentStatus = "paid";
    bill.totals = {400.0, 40.0, 20.0, 420.0};
    bill.lines = {
        {"MILK", 200.0, 20.0, 10.0, 1.0},
        {"BREAD", 200.0, 20.0, 10.0, 1.0}
    };

    TEST_ASSERT(repo.append(bill, &error), "Append bill failed: " + error);

    // Verify loaded content
    std::vector<Bill> loaded;
    TEST_ASSERT(repo.loadAll(loaded, &error), "Load bills failed: " + error);
    TEST_ASSERT(loaded.size() == 1, "Expected 1 aggregated bill");
    TEST_ASSERT(loaded[0].id == "261118001", "Bill ID mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(loaded[0].lines.size() == 2, "Expected 2 lines in aggregated bill");
    TEST_ASSERT(doubleNear(loaded[0].totals.payable, 420.0), "Payable mismatch");

    // Attempting to append duplicate bill ID should fail
    TEST_ASSERT(!repo.append(bill, &error), "Duplicate bill ID append must be rejected");
    TEST_ASSERT(error.find("already exists") != std::string::npos, "Unexpected error: " + error);

    // Clean up
    fs::remove_all(testDir, ec);
    return true;
}

} // namespace

    int totalTests = 0;
    int passedTests = 0;
    int failedTests = 0;

    std::cout << "========================================\n";
    std::cout << " BarcodeHub Billing Subsystem Unit Tests\n";
    std::cout << " Author: Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)\n";
    std::cout << "========================================\n\n";

    // Bill Calculator Tests
    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorSingleItem);
    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorMultipleItems);
    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorRoundingPrecision);
    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorEmptyLines);

    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorExcessiveDiscount);
    RUN_TEST(testCalculatorNonFiniteValues);

    // Payment Strategy Tests

    RUN_TEST(testCashPaymentInsufficient);
    RUN_TEST(testCardPaymentValid);
    RUN_TEST(testCardPaymentMissingBank);
    RUN_TEST(testCardPaymentInvalidCharacter);
    RUN_TEST(testWalletPaymentValid);

    std::cout << "\n----------------------------------------\n";
    std::cout << "Test Summary: " << passedTests << "/" << totalTests << " passed.\n";
    std::cout << "----------------------------------------\n";

    return (failedTests == 0) ? 0 : 1;
}

#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"
int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"
#include "product/ProductStorage.h"
#include "order/OrderManager.h"
#include "billing/BillingService.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <filesystem>
#include <iostream>

namespace fs = std::filesystem;
#define CHECK(x) do { if (!(x)) { std::cerr << "FAILED " << __LINE__ << ": " #x "\n"; return 1; } } while(0)
int main() {
    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    const auto dir = fs::temp_directory_path() / "barcodehub_checkout_test";
    fs::remove_all(dir);
    fs::create_directories(dir);
    bh::order::OrderManager orders(dir / "Order.txt", dir / "OrderDetail.txt");
    bh::billing::BillRepository bills(dir / "Bill.txt");
    Product product(1, "01001020", "MILK", "Fresh Milk", MeasurementUnit::Count, 250, 12.5, 10, 5, "-");
    bh::billing::BillingService service(orders, bills, [&](const std::string& id, bh::order::ProductSnapshot& out) {
        if (id != "1") return false;
        out = bh::integration::toSnapshot(product);
        return true;
    });
    std::string error;
    CHECK(orders.addToCart(bh::integration::toSnapshot(product), 2, &error));
    bh::order::Order created;
    CHECK(orders.createOrder("Walk-in Customer", "", created, &error));
    bh::billing::Bill preview;
    CHECK(service.preview(created.id, preview, &error));
    CHECK(std::abs(preview.totals.payable - 505.0) < 0.001);
    bh::billing::PaymentRequest invalid; invalid.amountTendered = 500;
    bh::billing::Bill result;
    CHECK(!service.checkout(created.id, invalid, result, nullptr, &error));
    invalid.amountTendered = 510;
    double change = 0;
    CHECK(service.checkout(created.id, invalid, result, &change, &error));
    CHECK(std::abs(change - 5) < 0.001);
    CHECK(result.id.size() == 9);
    CHECK(!service.checkout(created.id, invalid, result, nullptr, &error));
    std::vector<bh::billing::Bill> stored;
    CHECK(bills.loadAll(stored, &error));
    CHECK(stored.size() == 1 && stored[0].orderId == created.id);
    CHECK(std::abs(stored[0].totals.payable - 505) < 0.001);
    bh::order::Order paid;
    CHECK(orders.findOrder(created.id, paid, &error));
    CHECK(paid.status == bh::order::OrderStatus::Paid);
    fs::remove_all(dir);
    std::cout << "checkout OK\n";
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /tests/integration_test.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// End-to-end check of the backend flow: Product.txt -> find by barcode -> cart -> Order files.
// (The camera step is replaced by a typed barcode so this test needs no camera or OpenCV.)
#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"
#include "order/OrderManager.h"
#include "product/ProductStorage.h"

#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>

#define CHECK(cond)                                                              \
    do {                                                                         \
        if (!(cond)) {                                                           \
            std::cerr << "FAILED line " << __LINE__ << ": " #cond "\n";          \
            return EXIT_FAILURE;                                                 \
        }                                                                        \
    } while (0)

int main() {
    using namespace barcodehub;

    // 1. Product module loads a catalogue (same '#'-terminated layout as Product.txt).
    const auto productPath = std::filesystem::path("data/Product.txt");
    ProductManager catalogue;
    CHECK(catalogue.loadProducts(productPath.string()));
    CHECK(catalogue.getProducts().size() == 14);

    // 2. A scanned barcode finds the product.
    const Product* found = catalogue.searchByBarcode("01001020");
    CHECK(found != nullptr);

    // 3. The adapter hands it to the Order module, which fills the cart.
    const auto dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "barcodehub_integration";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(dir);
    order::OrderManager orders(dir / "Order.txt", dir / "OrderDetail.txt");
    std::string error;
    CHECK(orders.addToCart(integration::toSnapshot(*found), 3, &error));
    CHECK(!orders.addToCart(integration::toSnapshot(*found), 99, &error));   // more than in stock
    CHECK(orders.cart().size() == 1);
    // 3 units at product price plus tax minus discount
    CHECK(orders.totalPayable() > 989.99 && orders.totalPayable() < 990.01);

    // 4. The order is saved and can be read back.
    order::Order created;
    CHECK(orders.createOrder("Ali Khan", "", created, &error));
    CHECK(created.id.size() == 8);
    std::vector<order::OrderDetail> lines;
    CHECK(orders.loadOrderDetails(created.id, lines, &error));
    CHECK(lines.size() == 1 && lines[0].productBarcode == "01001020");

    // 5. Stock is reduced through the Product module.
    CHECK(catalogue.deductStock(found->getProductID(), 3));
    CHECK(catalogue.searchByBarcode("01001020")->getAvailableQuantity() == 39);

    std::filesystem::remove_all(dir);
    std::cout << "integration OK, order " << created.id << "\n";
    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /tests/OrderManagementSmokeTest.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Order Management tests. Plain checks (not assert) so they also run in Release builds.
#include "order/OrderManager.h"

#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>

using namespace barcodehub::order;
namespace fs = std::filesystem;

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond)                                                                   \
    do {                                                                              \
        if (!(cond)) {                                                                \
            ++failures;                                                               \
            std::cout << "FAIL line " << __LINE__ << ": " #cond "\n";                 \
        }                                                                             \
    } while (0)

static std::string readAll(const fs::path& path) {
    std::ifstream in(path);
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << in.rdbuf();
    return out.str();
}

static void writeAll(const fs::path& path, const std::string& text) {
    std::ofstream out(path, std::ios::trunc);
    out << text;
}

static ProductSnapshot milk() {
    ProductSnapshot p;
    p.productId = "1";
    p.barcode = "12345678";
    p.acronym = "MILK";
    p.name = "Fresh Milk";
    p.measurementUnit = "count";
    p.unitPrice = 250.0;
    p.taxPerUnit = 12.5;
    p.discountPerUnit = 10.0;
    p.availableQuantity = 5.0;
    return p;
}

static ProductSnapshot rice() {
    ProductSnapshot p;
    p.productId = "2";
    p.barcode = "87654321";
    p.acronym = "RICE";
    p.name = "Basmati Rice";
    p.measurementUnit = "weight";
    p.unitPrice = 400.0;
    p.taxPerUnit = 0.0;
    p.discountPerUnit = 0.0;
    p.availableQuantity = 10.0;
    return p;
}

int main() {
    const auto dir = fs::temp_directory_path() / "barcodehub_order_test";
    fs::remove_all(dir);
    fs::create_directories(dir);
    const auto orderFile = dir / "Order.txt";
    const auto detailFile = dir / "OrderDetail.txt";

    OrderManager m(orderFile, detailFile);
    std::string err;

    // ---- cart + stock validation ----
    CHECK(m.addToCart(milk(), 2, &err));
    CHECK(m.subtotalBeforeTax() == 500.0);
    CHECK(m.totalTax() == 25.0);
    CHECK(m.totalDiscount() == 20.0);
    CHECK(m.totalPayable() == 505.0);
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(milk(), 4, &err) && !err.empty());        // 2 + 4 > stock 5
    CHECK(m.cart().front().quantity == 2.0);                     // failed add changes nothing
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(milk(), 1.5, &err));                      // count needs whole number
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(milk(), 0, &err));                        // zero
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(milk(), -1, &err));                       // negative
    CHECK(m.addToCart(rice(), 1.5, &err));                       // weight allows decimals
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(rice(), 11, &err));                       // over stock
    CHECK(m.setCartQuantity("1", 5, &err));
    CHECK(!m.setCartQuantity("1", 6, &err));
    CHECK(!m.setCartQuantity("999", 1, &err));                   // not in cart
    CHECK(m.removeFromCart("2"));
    CHECK(!m.removeFromCart("2"));
    ProductSnapshot bad = milk();
    bad.discountPerUnit = 9999;
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(bad, 1, &err));                           // discount > price
    bad = milk();
    bad.barcode = "12 34";
    CHECK(!m.addToCart(bad, 1, &err));                           // space would break file format

    // ---- create order (names with spaces / special characters) ----
    Order o1;
    CHECK(m.createOrder("Ali Khan_Jr 100% #1", "", o1, &err));
    CHECK(o1.id.size() == 8);
    CHECK(o1.id.substr(6, 2) == o1.date.substr(8, 2));           // last two digits = day
    CHECK(o1.id.substr(4, 2) == "01");
    CHECK(m.cart().empty());

    const std::string orderText = readAll(orderFile);
    const std::string detailText = readAll(detailFile);
    CHECK(orderText.size() > 2 && orderText.substr(orderText.size() - 3) == " #\n");   // '#' ends the line
    CHECK(detailText == o1.id + " 1 12345678 5 #\n");
    CHECK(orderText.find("\"") == std::string::npos);            // no quotes in the file

    std::vector<Order> orders;
    CHECK(m.loadOrders(orders, &err));
    CHECK(orders.size() == 1);
    CHECK(orders[0].customerName == "Ali Khan_Jr 100% #1");     // lossless round trip
    CHECK(orders[0].customerContact.empty());
    CHECK(orders[0].status == OrderStatus::InProgress);

    CHECK(!m.createOrder("Someone", "", o1, &err));              // empty cart
    CHECK(m.addToCart(rice(), 2.25, &err));
    Order o2;
    CHECK(m.createOrder("Sara", "03001234567", o2, &err));
    CHECK(o2.id.substr(4, 2) == "02");                           // daily sequence increases
    CHECK(o2.id != o1.id);

    std::vector<OrderDetail> details;
    CHECK(m.loadOrderDetails(o2.id, details, &err));
    CHECK(details.size() == 1 && details[0].quantity == 2.25);

    // ---- update ----
    Order paid = o2;
    paid.status = OrderStatus::Paid;
    paid.paymentAmount = 900.0;
    paid.paymentMode = PaymentMode::Wallet;
    paid.paymentMerchant = "Jazz Cash";
    paid.date = "1999-01-01";                                    // must be ignored
    CHECK(m.updateOrder(paid, &err));
    Order reloaded;
    CHECK(m.findOrder(o2.id, reloaded, &err));
    CHECK(reloaded.paymentMerchant == "Jazz Cash");
    CHECK(reloaded.date == o2.date);
    Order zeroPaid = reloaded;
    zeroPaid.paymentAmount = 0;
    CHECK(!m.updateOrder(zeroPaid, &err));                       // paid but 0 amount
    Order ghost = reloaded;
    ghost.id = "99999999";
    CHECK(!m.updateOrder(ghost, &err));                          // unknown id
    Order archived = reloaded;
    archived.status = OrderStatus::Archived;
    CHECK(m.updateOrder(archived, &err));
    archived.status = OrderStatus::InProgress;
    CHECK(!m.updateOrder(archived, &err));                       // cannot reopen

    // ---- corrupted files are reported, not silently accepted ----
    writeAll(orderFile, "26110115 2026-11-15 14:25:10 Ali - in-progress 0.00 cash - \n");   // no '#'
    CHECK(!m.loadOrders(orders, &err) && err.find("'#'") != std::string::npos);
    writeAll(orderFile, "26110115 2026-11-15 14:25:10 Ali - shipped 0.00 cash - #\n");      // bad status
    CHECK(!m.loadOrders(orders, &err));
    writeAll(orderFile, "26110115 2026-11-15 14:25:10 Ali - paid 0.00 cash #\n");           // missing field
    CHECK(!m.loadOrders(orders, &err));
    writeAll(detailFile, "26110115 1 12345678 abc #\n");
    CHECK(!m.loadOrderDetails(details, &err));
    // hand-written valid file (e.g. from the instructor) with a blank line
    writeAll(orderFile, "26110115 2026-11-15 14:25:10 Ali_Khan 0300 paid 505.00 cash ABL #\n\n");
    CHECK(m.loadOrders(orders, &err) && orders.size() == 1 && orders[0].customerName == "Ali Khan");
    // empty files (as shipped in the repo) are fine
    writeAll(orderFile, "");
    writeAll(detailFile, "");
    CHECK(m.loadOrders(orders, &err) && orders.empty());
    CHECK(m.loadOrderDetails(details, &err) && details.empty());

    fs::remove_all(dir);
    if (failures == 0) std::cout << "Order Management tests: ALL PASSED\n";
    else std::cout << "Order Management tests: " << failures << " FAILED\n";
    return failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /tests/product_tests.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Unit tests for the product module. Plain C++17, no framework: run `product_tests` or `ctest`.
#include "product/ProductModel.h"
#include "product/ProductStorage.h"
#include "product/ProductValidation.h"
#include <cstdio>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <sstream>

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond)                                                                  \
    do                                                                               \
    {                                                                                \
        if (cond) std::cout << "PASS " << #cond << "\n";                             \
        else { std::cout << "FAIL " << #cond << "  (line " << __LINE__ << ")\n"; ++failures; } \
    } while (0)

static const char *kGood = "1 01001020 OLPMILK Olpers Full Cream Milk 1L count 340.00 0.00 10.00 42 data/barcodes/01001020.png#";

static ProductLoadResult loadText(const std::string &text)
{
    std::istringstream in(text);
    return ProductFileLoader().loadFrom(in);
}

int main()
{
    // ---- parser
    ProductParser parser;
    auto p = parser.parseLine(kGood);
    CHECK(p.ok());
    CHECK(p.product->getProductName() == "Olpers Full Cream Milk 1L");   // multi-word name
    CHECK(p.product->getAvailableQuantity() == 42 && p.product->getMeasurementUnit() == MeasurementUnit::Count);
    CHECK(!parser.parseLine("1 01001020 OLP Milk count 340 0 10 42 a.png").ok());          // no '#'
    CHECK(!parser.parseLine("1 01001020 OLP Milk each 340 0 10 42 a.png#").ok());          // bad unit
    CHECK(!parser.parseLine("1 01001020 OLP Milk count abc 0 10 42 a.png#").ok());         // bad number
    CHECK(!parser.parseLine("1 01001020 OLP count 340 0 10 42 a.png#").ok());              // too few fields

    // ---- validation + loader
    CHECK(loadText(std::string(kGood) + "\n").products.size() == 1);
    CHECK(loadText("1 01001020 TOOLONGACRO Milk count 340 0 10 42 a.png#\n").products.empty());   // acronym > 8
    CHECK(loadText("1 40001029 OLP Milk count 100 0 0 5 a.png#\n").products.empty());           // must start with 0
    CHECK(loadText("1 00001020 OLP Milk count 100 0 0 5 a.png#\n").products.empty());           // 00xxxxxx not allowed
    CHECK(loadText("1 01001020 OLP Milk count 100 0 0 5 a.png#\n").products.size() == 1);       // 01xxxxxx is fine
    CHECK(loadText("1 4000 OLP Milk count 340 0 10 42 a.png#\n").products.empty());              // barcode not 8 digits
    CHECK(loadText("1 01001020 OLP Milk count -5 0 0 42 a.png#\n").products.empty());            // negative price
    CHECK(loadText("1 01001020 OLP Milk count 100 0 150 42 a.png#\n").products.empty());         // discount > price
    CHECK(loadText("0 01001020 OLP Milk count 100 0 0 42 a.png#\n").products.empty());           // id 0
    auto dup = loadText(std::string(kGood) + "\n2 01001020 OTHER Other count 10 0 0 1 b.png#\n");
    CHECK(dup.products.size() == 1 && dup.errorCount() == 1);                                   // duplicate barcode
    auto mixed = loadText(std::string(kGood) + "\ngarbage line\n\n3 01002140 BRD Bread count 260 18 0 5 b.png#\n");
    CHECK(mixed.products.size() == 2 && mixed.rejectedLines == 1);                              // good lines survive
    CHECK(loadText("1 01001021 OLP Milk count 100 0 0 5 a.png#\n").warningCount() == 1);       // bad check digit = warning only
    CHECK(!ProductFileLoader().load("no/such/file.txt").fileOpened);

    // ---- open/closed: a new rule is added without editing the validator
    struct NoMilkRule : IProductRule
    {
        void check(const Product &pr, ValidationReport &r) const override
        {
            if (pr.getProductName().find("Milk") != std::string::npos) r.errors.push_back("milk not allowed");
        }
    };
    auto v = ProductValidator::standard();
    v.addRule(std::make_unique<NoMilkRule>());
    CHECK(!v.validate(*p.product).ok());

    // ---- manager: search + stock
    ProductManager pm;
    CHECK(pm.loadProducts("data/Product.txt"));
    CHECK(pm.getProducts().size() == 14);
    CHECK(pm.searchByBarcode("01001020") && pm.searchByBarcode("01001020")->getProductID() == 1);
    CHECK(pm.searchByBarcode("12345678") == nullptr);
    CHECK(pm.searchByID(5) && pm.searchByID(5)->getAcronym() == "BASMATI");
    CHECK(pm.searchByID(99) == nullptr);
    CHECK(pm.searchByCode("olpmilk") && pm.searchByCode("7") && pm.searchByCode("01001020"));
    CHECK(pm.searchByText("tea").size() == 1);
    CHECK(pm.deductStock(1, 2) && pm.searchByID(1)->getAvailableQuantity() == 40);
    CHECK(!pm.deductStock(1, 9999) && pm.searchByID(1)->getAvailableQuantity() == 40);

    // ---- stock validator + swappable policy
    const Product eggs = *pm.searchByID(7); // 4 in stock
    StockValidator sv;
    CHECK(sv.check(eggs, 4).ok());
    CHECK(sv.check(eggs, 5).status == StockStatus::InsufficientStock);
    CHECK(sv.checkAdd(eggs, 3, 1).ok() && !sv.checkAdd(eggs, 4, 1).ok());
    CHECK(sv.check(eggs, 0).status == StockStatus::InvalidQuantity);
    CHECK(sv.check(*pm.searchByID(11), 1).status == StockStatus::OutOfStock);
    struct BelowAvailable : IStockPolicy {
        bool allows(int requested, int available) const override { return requested < available; }
        std::string describe() const override { return "strictly below available"; }
    };
    StockValidator strict(std::make_shared<BelowAvailable>());
    CHECK(!strict.check(eggs, 4).ok() && strict.check(eggs, 3).ok());

    // ---- description (what) + formatter (how)
    ProductDescriber d(10);
    CHECK(d.describe(eggs).stockLevel == "low" && d.describe(*pm.searchByID(11)).stockLevel == "out");
    const std::string text = TextProductFormatter().format(d.describe(eggs));
    CHECK(text.find("Farm Fresh Eggs Dozen") != std::string::npos && text.find("PKR 390.00") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(formatPKR(1234567.5) == "PKR 1234567.50" && formatPKR(0) == "PKR 0.00");

    // ---- writer round trip
    const std::string tmp = "product_roundtrip.txt";
    ProductManager a, b;
    a.loadProducts("data/Product.txt");
    a.deductStock(1, 2);
    ProductFileWriter w;
    CHECK(w.save(tmp, a.getProducts()));
    CHECK(b.loadProducts(tmp) && b.getProducts().size() == a.getProducts().size() &&
          b.searchByID(1)->getAvailableQuantity() == 40);
    std::remove(tmp.c_str());

    std::cout << (failures ? "FAILED" : "ALL OK") << " (" << failures << " failures)\n";
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}

FILE: /tests/regression.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "barcode/BarcodeGenerator.h"
#include "barcode/Decoder.h"
#include "scanner/ImageScanner.h"
#include "scanner/CameraScanner.h"
#include "scanner/Scanner.h"
#include "mainwindow.h"
#include "generatepage.h"
#include "decodepage.h"
#include "widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QImageReader>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QThread>
#include <QTransform>
#include <iostream>
#include <functional>

static int failures = 0;
static void check(bool ok, const QString &name) {
    std::cout << (ok ? "PASS " : "FAIL ") << name.toStdString() << std::endl;
    if (!ok) ++failures;
}

static void roundTrip(const QImage &image, const QString &code, const QString &name) {
    const auto r = ImageScanner().scan(image);
    check(r.success && r.text == code, name + (r.success ? "" : ": " + r.error));
}

static bool waitFor(const std::function<bool()> &condition, int timeoutMs = 10000) {
    QElapsedTimer timer;
    timer.start();
    while (!condition() && timer.elapsed() < timeoutMs) {
        QApplication::processEvents();
        QThread::msleep(10);
    }
    return condition();
}

static int cameraSmoke() {
    const auto names = CameraScanner::availableCameras();
    check(!names.isEmpty(), "camera enumeration");
    for (const auto &name : names) std::cout << "DEVICE " << name.toStdString() << std::endl;
    if (names.isEmpty()) return 1;
    CameraScanner camera;
    for (int cycle = 0; cycle < 2; ++cycle) {
        QString error;
        const bool opened = camera.openCamera(0, &error);
        check(opened, "open camera cycle " + QString::number(cycle) + ": " + error);
        if (!opened) continue;
        int frames = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) {
            const auto frame = camera.readFrame();
            if (frame.success && !frame.frame.isNull()) ++frames;
        }
        check(frames == 20, "20 real frames with decoder enabled");
        camera.closeCamera();
        check(!camera.isOpened(), "camera released");
    }
    {
        MainWindow window;
        auto *decode = window.decodePage();
        decode->setCameraActive(true);
        decode->cameraStartRequested(0);
        check(waitFor([&] { return !decode->cameraView()->frame().isNull(); }), "threaded UI receives real camera frames");
        decode->cameraView()->setFrozen(true);
        const auto frozen = decode->cameraView()->frame().toImage();
        QElapsedTimer delay; delay.start();
        waitFor([&] { return delay.elapsed() > 400; }, 1000);
        check(frozen == decode->cameraView()->frame().toImage(), "live freeze preserves frame");
        decode->stopCamera();
        check(!decode->cameraActive() && decode->cameraView()->frame().isNull(), "UI stop clears camera state");
        // Stop immediately while the worker is opening: late callbacks must not restart it.
        decode->setCameraActive(true);
        decode->cameraStartRequested(0);
        decode->stopCamera();
        delay.restart();
        waitFor([&] { return delay.elapsed() > 1500; }, 2500);
        check(!decode->cameraActive() && decode->cameraView()->frame().isNull(), "cancel opening ignores stale callbacks");
    }
    QString error;
    check(camera.openCamera(0, &error), "window shutdown releases camera for reuse");
    camera.closeCamera();
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    if (app.arguments().contains("--camera-smoke")) return cameraSmoke();
    BarcodeGenerator generator;
    for (const QString &bad : {QString(), QString("012345"), QString("12345"), QString("1234567"), QString("12a456")})
        check(!generator.generate(bad).success, "reject invalid input: " + bad);
    check(BarcodeGenerator::ean8Payload("123456") == "01234565", "known EAN-8 check digit");

    for (const QString &code : {QString("100000"), QString("123456"), QString("749205"), QString("999999")}) {
        for (bool digits : {false, true}) {
            const auto result = generator.generate(code, digits);
            check(result.success, "generate " + code);
            roundTrip(result.image, code, "round trip " + code + (digits ? " with text" : " without text"));
        }
    }
    const QImage original = generator.generate("749205").image;
#if QT_VERSION >= QT_VERSION_CHECK(6, 0, 0)
    roundTrip(original.flipped(Qt::Horizontal), "749205", "mirrored barcode");
#else
    roundTrip(original.mirrored(true, false), "749205", "mirrored barcode");
#endif
    roundTrip(original.transformed(QTransform().rotate(90)), "749205", "vertical barcode");
    roundTrip(original.transformed(QTransform().rotate(180)), "749205", "upside down barcode");
    roundTrip(original.scaled(300, 120, Qt::IgnoreAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation), "749205", "small barcode");
    roundTrip(original.scaled(1800, 720), "749205", "large barcode");
    QImage tilted(900, 500, QImage::Format_RGB32);
    tilted.fill(Qt::white);
    { QPainter p(&tilted); p.translate(450, 250); p.rotate(12); p.drawImage(-300, -120, original); }
    roundTrip(tilted, "749205", "12 degree tilted barcode");
    QImage transparent = original.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_ARGB32);
    for (int y = 0; y < transparent.height(); ++y)
        for (int x = 0; x < transparent.width(); ++x)
            if (qGray(transparent.pixel(x, y)) > 245) transparent.setPixel(x, y, qRgba(0, 0, 0, 0));
    roundTrip(transparent, "749205", "transparent background");
    QTemporaryDir dir;
    const QString path = dir.filePath(QString::fromUtf8("barcode_\xE6\xB5\x8B\xE8\xAF\x95.png"));
    check(original.save(path), "save PNG at Unicode path");
    roundTrip(QImage(path), "749205", "reload saved PNG");
    const QString jpeg = dir.filePath("barcode.jpg");
    check(original.save(jpeg, "JPG", 75), "save JPEG");
    roundTrip(QImage(jpeg), "749205", "JPEG decode");
    QImage blank(600, 240, QImage::Format_RGB32); blank.fill(Qt::white);
    check(!ImageScanner().scan(blank).success, "reject blank image");
    check(!ImageScanner().scan(QImage()).success, "reject empty image");
    QImage damaged = original;
    { QPainter p(&damaged); p.fillRect(250, 0, 30, damaged.height(), Qt::white); }
    check(!ImageScanner().scan(damaged).success, "reject damaged bars");
    // Replace only the check digit with another valid pattern: structure still passes.
    QImage wrongCheck = generator.generate("123456", false).image;
    { QPainter p(&wrongCheck); p.fillRect(464, 26, 49, 188, Qt::white);
      const QString bits = "1110010"; // right-hand encoding of zero, instead of five
      for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i) if (bits[i] == '1') p.fillRect(464 + 7*i, 26, 7, 188, Qt::black); }
    check(!ImageScanner().scan(wrongCheck).success, "reject wrong checksum");
    std::string stale = "123456";
    Decoder decoder;
    check(!decoder.decodeSix("bad", stale) && stale.empty(), "failed decode clears previous output");

    // Exercise the actual frontend-to-backend signal connections.
    {
        MainWindow window;
        auto *decode = window.decodePage();
        auto *history = decode->findChild<QTableWidget *>("scanHistory");
        check(history && history->rowCount() == 0, "history starts empty");
        window.generatePage()->setCode("749205");
        auto *view = window.generatePage()->findChild<BarcodeView *>();
        roundTrip(view->renderBarcode().toImage(), "749205", "UI generated image decodes");
        decode->sampleRequested("749205", false);
        check(history->rowCount() == 1 && history->item(0, 2)->text() == "749205", "sample uses real decoder and logs result");
        decode->sampleRequested("749205", true);
        check(history->rowCount() == 1, "failed sample does not add success history");
        decode->setTab(2);
        for (auto *tabs : decode->findChildren<SegBar *>()) check(tabs->current() == 2, "tabs stay synchronized");
        decode->setCameraList({});
        check(!decode->cameraActive(), "no camera is inactive");
        CameraView camera;
        camera.setRunning(true);
        camera.setFrame(QPixmap::fromImage(original));
        camera.setFrozen(true);
        camera.setFrame(QPixmap::fromImage(blank));
        check(camera.frame().toImage() == original, "freeze retains displayed frame");
        camera.setRunning(false);
        check(camera.frame().isNull() && !camera.frozen(), "stop clears frame and frozen state");
    }
    std::cout << "Failures: " << failures << std::endl;
    return failures ? 1 : 0;
}

FILE: /tests/scanner_tests.cpp

// Unit tests for the barcode decoder (pure C++, no camera): pictures are drawn in memory.
#include "decoder.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(c) do { if (!(c)) { std::printf("FAILED line %d: %s\n", __LINE__, #c); ++failures; } } while (0)

static std::string modulesFor(const std::string &d)
{
    std::string m = "101";
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) m += Decoder::patterns[d[i] - '0'];
    m += "01010";
    for (int i = 4; i < 8; ++i)
        for (const char *p = Decoder::patterns[d[i] - '0']; *p; ++p) m += (*p == '1') ? '0' : '1';
    return m + "101";
}

// Draw the barcode `scale` pixels per module, with quiet zones, inside a larger picture.
static GrayImage draw(const std::string &digits, int scale, int margin, bool upsideDown = false, bool sideways = false, int noise = 0)
{
    std::string m = modulesFor(digits);
    if (upsideDown) m.assign(m.rbegin(), m.rend());
    const int w = static_cast<int>(m.size()) * scale + 2 * margin, h = 120;
    GrayImage g;
    g.width = sideways ? h : w;
    g.height = sideways ? w : h;
    g.pixels.assign(static_cast<std::size_t>(g.width) * g.height, 235);
    std::srand(7);
    for (int x = 0; x < w; ++x)
    {
        const int mi = (x - margin) / scale;
        const bool dark = x >= margin && mi >= 0 && mi < static_cast<int>(m.size()) && m[static_cast<std::size_t>(mi)] == '1';
        for (int y = 20; y < 100; ++y)
        {
            int v = dark ? 20 : 235;
            if (noise) v += std::rand() % (2 * noise + 1) - noise;
            v = v < 0 ? 0 : (v > 255 ? 255 : v);
            const int px = sideways ? y : x, py = sideways ? x : y;
            g.pixels[static_cast<std::size_t>(py) * g.width + px] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(v);
        }
    }
    return g;
}

int main()
{
    Decoder d;
    std::string out;
    CHECK(Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit("40001029"));
    CHECK(!Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit("40001028"));

    for (const char *code : {"40001029", "40002149", "40003511", "96385074", "00000000"})
    {
        if (!Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit(code)) continue;
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 2, 30), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 5, 10), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20, true), out) && out == code);        // upside down
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20, false, true), out) && out == code); // sideways
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 4, 20, false, false, 18), out) && out == code); // camera noise
    }
    // barcode only in the lower part of the picture: found when the aiming line is moved onto it
    {
        GrayImage g = draw("40001029", 3, 20);
        for (int y = 0; y < 60; ++y)                       // blank out the top half of the bars
            for (int x = 0; x < g.width; ++x) g.pixels[static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * g.width + x] = 235;
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(g, out, 0.8) && out == "40001029");
    }
    // wrong check digit: not accepted, but reported
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(draw("40001028", 3, 20), out));
    CHECK(d.isBadCheck());
    // blank picture
    GrayImage blank; blank.width = 200; blank.height = 100; blank.pixels.assign(20000, 200);
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(blank, out));
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(GrayImage{}, out));

    if (failures) { std::printf("%d scanner test(s) FAILED\n", failures); return 1; }
    std::printf("scanner tests OK\n");
    return 0;
}

FILE: /cameraScanner.cpp

#include "cameraScanner.h"
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
#include <opencv2/videoio.hpp>
#if __has_include(<opencv2/geometry.hpp>)
#include <opencv2/geometry.hpp>
#endif
using namespace std;
cameraScanner::cameraScanner(int idx, int width, int height)
{
    index = idx;
    this->width = width;
    this->height = height;
}
// Open the camera
bool cameraScanner::openCamera()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    camera.open(index, cv::CAP_DSHOW);
#else
    camera.open(index);
#endif
    if (!camera.isOpened())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera cannot open";
        return false;
    }
    // produces an MJPG of 640x480 screen
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FOURCC, cv::VideoWriter::fourcc('M', 'J', 'P', 'G'));
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, width);
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, height);
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FPS, 30);
    return true;
}
// locate the barcode
bool cameraScanner::find(const cv::Mat &gray, cv::Rect &box) const
{
    cv::Mat gx, gy, score, mask;
    cv::Sobel(gray, gx, CV_32F, 1, 0, 3);
    cv::Sobel(gray, gy, CV_32F, 0, 1, 3);
    score = cv::max(cv::abs(gx) - cv::abs(gy), 0.0);
    score.convertTo(score, CV_8U, 0.25);
    // find the blob
    cv::blur(score, score, cv::Size(9, 9));
    cv::threshold(score, mask, 0, 255, cv::THRESH_BINARY | cv::THRESH_OTSU);
    cv::Mat k = cv::getStructuringElement(cv::MORPH_RECT, cv::Size(21, 7));
    cv::morphologyEx(mask, mask, cv::MORPH_CLOSE, k);
    cv::erode(mask, mask, cv::Mat(), cv::Point(-1, -1), 4);
    cv::dilate(mask, mask, cv::Mat(), cv::Point(-1, -1), 4);
    vector<vector<cv::Point>> contours;
    cv::findContours(mask, contours, cv::RETR_EXTERNAL, cv::CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE);
    cv::Rect Best;
    double bestArea = 0;
    for (const vector<cv::Point> &c : contours)
    {
        double a = cv::contourArea(c);
        if (a > bestArea)
        {
            bestArea = a;
            Best = cv::boundingRect(c);
        }
    }
    if (bestArea > 2000 && Best.width > Best.height && Best.height > 40)
    {
        box = Best;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}
// read each frame and locate the barcode in grayscale
bool cameraScanner::frameRead(std::string &six, cv::Rect &box, const cv::Mat &frame)
{
    box = cv::Rect();
    six.clear();
    errorMsg.clear();
    if (frame.empty())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
        return false;
    }
    cv::Mat gray;
    if (frame.channels() == 1)
        gray = frame;
    else if (frame.channels() == 3)
        cv::cvtColor(frame, gray, cv::COLOR_BGR2GRAY);
    else if (frame.channels() == 4)
        cv::cvtColor(frame, gray, cv::COLOR_BGRA2GRAY);
    else
        return false;
    // Decode across several scan lines before relying on the contour detector.
    if (decoder.decodeCropping(gray, six))
    {
        cv::Rect detected;
        box = find(gray, detected) ? detected : cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
        return true;
    }
    // barcode will be located on the grayscale
    cv::Rect Best;
    if (!find(gray, Best))
    {
        cv::Mat rotated;
        cv::rotate(gray, rotated, cv::ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE);
        if (decoder.decodeCropping(rotated, six))
        {
            box = cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    box = Best;
    cv::Rect pad(Best.x - 15, Best.y, Best.width + 30, Best.height);
    pad &= cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
    cv::Mat barcode = gray(pad).clone();
    return decoder.decodeCropping(barcode, six);
}
// scanning the located barcode for the six digits
bool cameraScanner::scan(std::string &sixDigits)
{
    sixDigits.clear();
    if (!camera.isOpened() && !openCamera())
        return false;
    const auto start = cv::getTickCount();
    // tick count
    while ((cv::getTickCount() - start) / cv::getTickFrequency() < 30.0)
    {
        cv::Mat frame;
        if (!camera.read(frame) || frame.empty())
        {
            errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
            camera.release();
            return false;
        }
        cv::Rect box;
        if (frameRead(sixDigits, box, frame))
        {
            camera.release();
            return true;
        }
    }
    camera.release();
    errorMsg = "No barcode found within 30 seconds";
    return false;
}
// store the error message
std::string cameraScanner::errMessage() const
{
    return errorMsg;
}
// grab one frame from the camera (the GUI calls this on every timer tick)
bool cameraScanner::readFrame(cv::Mat &frame)
{
    if (!camera.isOpened() || !camera.read(frame) || frame.empty())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
// close the camera
cameraScanner::~cameraScanner()
{
    camera.release();
}
// release the camera when the GUI stops scanning
void cameraScanner::closeCamera()
{
    camera.release();
}
// did the last frame show a barcode with a wrong check digit?
bool cameraScanner::badCheckDigit() const
{
    return decoder.isBadCheck();
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /cameraScanner.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include "decoder.h"
#include <opencv2/videoio.hpp>
#include <string>
class cameraScanner
{
    cv::VideoCapture camera;
    Decoder decoder;
    int index;
    int width;
    int height;
    std::string errorMsg;
    bool find(const cv::Mat &gray, cv::Rect &box) const;

public:
    cameraScanner(int idx = 0, int width = 640, int height = 480);
    bool openCamera();
    bool readFrame(cv::Mat &frame);
    bool scan(std::string &six);
    bool frameRead(std::string &six, cv::Rect &box, const cv::Mat &frame);
    std::string errMessage() const;
    ~cameraScanner();
    void closeCamera();         // release camera when scanning stops
    bool badCheckDigit() const; // did the last frame have a wrong check digit?
};

# Changelog

All notable changes to **BarcodeHub** are documented in this file.

## [3.0.0] — Increment 3: Inventory, Discounts, Reporting & Advanced Billing

### Planned

- Add product category management through the GUI, stored in `PCategory.txt`.
- Use the first two digits of every barcode as the product category.
- Store the category ID of each product in `Product.txt`.
- Allow managers to generate barcodes and to add or edit products.
- Add an inventory view and inventory updates for managers.
- Update available product quantities with every order.
- Allow existing orders to be opened and edited; paid orders only within 7 days of payment and after manager approval.
- Reduce the clicks needed to record an order, put an order on hold, and resume an on-hold order.
- Extend `Bill.txt` with amount received, balance returned, seasonal discount, special discount, bill date, and bill time.
- Introduce `BillDetail.txt` to store the product lines of each bill.
- Introduce `Seasons.txt` for seasonal discounts, and allow managers to add discounts and set them active or inactive.
- Apply the seasonal discount automatically when the bill date falls within an active season.
- Support special discounts of up to 8 percent.
- Print bills as PDF for cashiers and managers.
- Add management reports: incomplete orders, completed orders, cash sales, all sales, top ten most sold items, and top ten items by remaining stock.
- Provide separate cashier and manager features in the user interface.

### Development Notes

- Increment 3 builds on the Point of Sale system from Increment 2.
- Requirements and open design decisions are documented in `Project-Planning/Increment-3.md`.
- Increment 3 is currently in the planning stage. None of the items listed above are implemented yet.

## [2.0.0] — Increment 2: Point of Sale System

### Planned

- Introduce a GUI-based Point of Sale (POS) workflow.
- Implement product management using `Product.txt`.
- Add product lookup by barcode and product ID.
- Display product specifications and validate available stock.
- Integrate camera-based barcode scanning with the POS.
- Implement cart and order management.
- Generate unique 8-digit Order IDs.
- Store order records in `Order.txt`.
- Store product details and quantities in `OrderDetail.txt`.
- Implement bill generation and payment calculations.
- Save generated bills in `Bill.txt`.
- Support payment modes: cash, card, and wallet.

### Development Notes

- Increment 2 extends the existing barcode generator and reader from Increment 1.
- The system is being developed using modular components to promote separation of responsibilities, reusability, and maintainability.
- Increment 2 is currently in development.

## [1.0.0] — Increment 1: Initial Project Setup

### Added

- Established the initial project structure and directory organization.
- Configured CMake as the project build system.
- Implemented the Qt Widgets-based desktop application foundation.
- Created the initial application window and navigation.
- Implemented barcode generation for six-digit numeric input.
- Implemented barcode reading functionality.
- Added the Decode Barcode interface with camera and image-upload sections.
- Introduced the initial Scan Logs interface.
- Organized backend modules for barcode generation, reading, and scanning.
- Added project documentation and contribution guidelines.

### Notes

- Established the foundational barcode generation and reading functionality.
- Increment 1 serves as the base for the POS capabilities introduced in Increment 2.

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.21)

# Windows + Ninja: Qt Creator sometimes hands CMake Visual Studio's cl.exe together with a MinGW
# Qt kit, which cannot compile. Unless an MSVC Qt is being used, switch to Qt's own MinGW g++.
if(WIN32 AND NOT CMAKE_GENERATOR MATCHES "Visual Studio"
   AND (NOT CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER OR CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER MATCHES "[cC][lL]\\.exe$")
   AND NOT "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}${Qt6_DIR}${QT_DIR}" MATCHES "[mM][sS][vV][cC]")
    include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/cmake/toolchain-qt-mingw.cmake")
endif()

project(BarcodePOS LANGUAGES CXX)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

if(MSVC)
    # Qt 6 needs a correct __cplusplus value; also read sources as UTF-8.
    add_compile_options(/Zc:__cplusplus /utf-8 /permissive-)
endif()

option(BACKEND_ONLY "Build backend and tests without Qt" OFF)

# Camera support uses Qt Multimedia (shipped with Qt; no OpenCV needed):
#   AUTO (default) - camera if the Qt Multimedia component is installed, otherwise built without it
#   ON             - camera required: configuring fails if Qt Multimedia is missing
#   OFF            - no camera (typed barcode lookup only)
set(CAMERA_MODE AUTO CACHE STRING "Camera: AUTO, ON, OFF")
set_property(CACHE CAMERA_MODE PROPERTY STRINGS AUTO ON OFF)
# Which library grabs the pictures (the barcode decoder itself is always the same pure C++ code):
#   AUTO   - OpenCV if a build matching the compiler is found, otherwise Qt Multimedia
#   QT     - Qt Multimedia only
#   OPENCV - OpenCV only (set OpenCV_DIR; it must be built for the SAME compiler as Qt:
#            the official OpenCV Windows download is MSVC-only -> use it with the MSVC kit)
set(CAMERA_BACKEND AUTO CACHE STRING "Camera library: AUTO, QT, OPENCV")
set_property(CACHE CAMERA_BACKEND PROPERTY STRINGS AUTO QT OPENCV)

# ============================================================
# BACKEND LIBRARIES
# ============================================================

# Product management
add_library(product_core STATIC
    Backend/src/product/ProductModel.cpp
    Backend/src/product/ProductStorage.cpp
    Backend/src/product/ProductValidation.cpp
)

target_include_directories(product_core PUBLIC
    Backend/include

)

)

target_include_directories(common_core PUBLIC
    Backend/include
)

    Backend/src/billing/BillCalculator.cpp
    Backend/src/billing/BillingService.cpp
    Backend/src/billing/BillRecordCodec.cpp
    Backend/src/billing/BillRepository.cpp
    Backend/src/billing/PaymentMethod.cpp
)

target_include_directories(billing_core PUBLIC
    Backend/include
)

target_link_libraries(billing_core PUBLIC
    order_core
    common_core
)

add_library(integration_core STATIC
    Backend/src/integration/ProductAdapter.cpp

    Backend/include

    product_core
